## 2026-07-10 → 07-14 — pedagogical blocks, Phase 2.6 grading, editor design/UX pass (STATE summaries, moved 2026-07-17)

- **Editor design pass — color tokens + contrast + block accents** (2026-07-14, app + renderer; needs `publish-activity` redeploy — see Pending #2) — three `/impeccable` items on the editor surface. (1) **Color tokens + contrast** (`bedb1ca`, app-only): `editor.css` was ~200 raw `rgb()` literals with no token layer; introduced a two-layer `:root` system (primitives `--ed-slate/blue/indigo/amber/…` → semantic `--ed-surface/-text-*/-accent*/-warning*/-danger*/…`), every rule routed through it. Baked the WCAG-AA fix in: `--ed-text-muted` = slate-500 (was the failing slate-400 ~2.8:1) — the block-picker heading/empty/placeholder text and the image path now hit ≥4.5:1; slate-400 (`--ed-faint`) is non-text only. (2) **Block accents de-striped** (`2d00e9d`, app + renderer): the side-stripe (`border-left: 3-4px`) on the three instructional/response block-type keys → leading type glyph + full 1px tinted hairline + tint wash (objectives `◎` indigo, self-explanation `❝` teal, free-response `¶` amber), adding a non-color/grayscale cue. Prompt-led blocks (self-explanation, free-response) gained a small authoring-only type-label header; selected state keeps each block's tint + adds only the blue outline. Renderer parity for `learning_objectives` only (the others have no published stripe). **Deliberately left:** callout / MC-feedback / free-text-feedback / solution-reveal panels (separate annotation pattern; callouts encode variant via border-style for print) + the blockquote left rule (standard typography). Verified: 559 renderer + 570 app tests green, typecheck clean, computed styles confirmed live (no stripe, correct tints/icons); bundle regenerated + committed.
- **Add-block UX rebuild + blanks "More options" + column-aware insertion** (2026-07-14, app-only, no wire/deploy) — author's friend flagged editor cognitive load; worked from a new reusable UX-review instrument [docs/design/ux-lens.md](docs/design/ux-lens.md). (1) **Blank popover**: acceptable-answers + "Accept in any order" (renamed from "Interchangeable…") collapsed under a "+ More options" disclosure; numeric explainer shows only when numeric is on. (2) **Block insertion rebuilt**: the activity toolbar's "+ Insert" dropdown is GONE (reference panel keeps its dropdown) — replaced by a centered two-pane **"Add a block" window** (`BlockInsertModal`): alphabetical category rail (Layout preselected) + search, driven by the shared `slashMenuItems`. Opened by a hover **insert line** at the top of the block under the cursor (spans the containing column/cell width; inserts ABOVE it) + a persistent **end square** (appends; also the sole affordance on an empty doc). First insert into an empty doc drops the leftover empty paragraph. (3) **Columns-aware**: the line reaches INSIDE cells (depth-aware target — walks up to the block whose parent is doc or `column`); new `topLevelOnly` flag on the structural blocks (Section break, 2/3 columns) disables them in the window AND hides them in the `/` menu when nested (inserting one at a cell position was splitting the columns block). (4) **`/` discoverability**: new dependency-free `PlaceholderHint` extension shows grey "Type / to add a block…" on empty top-level lines — the signifier that made removing the toolbar insert safe. Typecheck + **570 app tests** green. Browser-verified on `/playground`: window structure, insert-above, end square, empty-doc hint+square+cleanup, cell insert (`parent==column`), cell-illegal disabled/hidden, line width (742 full-page / cell-width in a cell), `/` shows 23 items in a cell vs 26 top-level.
- **Math fields: auto-focus on insert + keyboard caret handoff** (2026-07-14, app-only, `fcc43fc`) — author-flagged UX: math atoms are `atom:true` with a MathLive `<math-field>` interior, so entering edit mode is a deliberate handoff that used to need a mouse click. Now (1) **insert** (toolbar `fx` / Insert menu / slash) auto-opens the field **focused** with the seed LaTeX selected (first keystroke replaces it); (2) **Enter** on a selected math node, and **Arrow{Left,Right}** stepping onto an inline math atom from the doc, open the field at the matching end; (3) MathLive's **`move-out`** (arrow/tab past an edge) + **Escape** hand the caret back to the doc on the correct side. Mechanism: a small `MathFocus` ProseMirror plugin carries a one-shot "open" signal as a **node decoration** (nonce'd — never cleared, because removing a node decoration redraws the node and blurs the just-focused field; a new open replaces the set); the two near-duplicate NodeViews collapsed into one shared `useMathFieldEditing` hook that consumes it, focuses, and places the caret. Opening is always an explicit signal, never a raw NodeSelection, so select-then-delete still works. No schema/renderer/runtime change → **no wire/storage bump, no deploy artifact** (rides the SPA). **Browser-verified on `/playground`:** inline + block insert both open focused with seed selected (`[[0,4]]`/`[[0,8]]`), type replaces seed, ArrowLeft into an existing formula re-enters at the end (`[[3,3]]`), move-out closes back to the doc. Typecheck + 570 app tests green. (Verification gotchas worth not rediscovering: a 0-height Browser-pane viewport and `requestAnimationFrame` throttling under JS-driven clicks both masquerade as focus failures — use a sized window + real mouse clicks. Scope note: the inline rich-text editors (hint/feedback/solution/definition popovers) don't register `MathFocus`, so math there still needs a click — harmless, extendable later.)
- **Editor UX: "+ Insert" dropdown reorg + faded-example step numbering** (2026-07-13) — two author-flagged UX fixes. (1) **Insert dropdown** ([InsertMenu.tsx](packages/app/src/editor/components/InsertMenu.tsx)) had grown to 20 two-line rows in one scrolling column; added a **type-to-filter field** (reuses the slash menu's exact title+keywords match; combobox keyboard model — input keeps focus, arrows move highlight, Enter inserts), **subgroup** headings (Structure → Layout/Instructional/Media & figures; Questions → Blanks/Choice & drag/Graphing/Free response), and a **lucide-react icon** per block type in a left gutter. New optional `subgroup`+`icon` fields on `SlashMenuItem` keep the shared list the single source of truth; a test pins "every Insert-dropdown item has an icon". App-only. **Browser-verified on `/playground`.** Committed `8c1bd12`. (2) **Faded/guided-practice numbering — full rebuild** (author-ruled; reverses the 2026-07-12 "steps number as ordinary problems" call — see [DECISIONS](docs/DECISIONS.md) → "Faded worked example — box is one problem"). The box is now ONE numbered problem (number leads the title, e.g. "3. ✍ Guided practice"); faded fill_in_blank steps are lettered (a)/(b) **locally**, gutter-less (run-in inline label), so they no longer steal worksheet numbers and reclaim writing/print width. Touches schema (1 field), renderer (`isNumberedBlock`, `renderFadedWorkedExample`, `renderFillInBlank` fadedStep mode, new `step-letter.ts`, styles) + editor (`problemNumberAt` treats the box atomic + `fadedStepContextAt`, FillInBlankView/FadedWorkedExampleView, editor.css). Editor reactivity gotcha handled: child renders the letter always, parent hides via `is-labels-off` CSS (a child NodeView can't see a parent attr change). Display-only → **no wire/storage bump**; scoring rides the child blanks. **Browser-verified on `/playground`:** box "1." leads the title, "(a)" inline, a standalone problem after it correctly numbers "2.", and the toggle hides/shows labels live. ## 2026-07-12 — number_line + data_plot trains, import fences, fixes (moved from STATE 2026-07-12 drift-audit)

All entries below shipped, deployed, and author-confirmed 2026-07-12; durable per-feature status is STATE.md's Status-by-area table. Deploy clearances follow the narratives.

**▶ `data_plot` GRADED BUILDS (histogram + box) — all 5 GB slices.** Kit `V6T6HAUS` on R2 (manifest `a0f2d9d`), `ingest-submission` v36 (widened `DataPlotResponse` union, `verify_jwt:false`, MCP-verified) + `publish-activity` v88 live. A teacher can author + publish a graded histogram or box plot; the graded data_plot block covers all three stats charts. The two graded constructions: **`build_histogram`** (the student drags each bar's frequency) + **`build_boxplot`** (drag five tick-snapping handles clamped between neighbours; scored within `tolerance` vs the TI-84 exclusive-median five-number summary — method LOCKED). Design + 9 approved decisions in the follow-up section of [design/data-plot-block.md](design/data-plot-block.md). **Additive — NO wire/storage bump** (new `DataPlotResponse` union members, the graph ray/segment precedent). Built: schema+response union `e6bb062`; pure `scoreHistogram`/`scoreBoxplot` + two dedicated SVG boards + `mountDataPlotQuestion` branch `58fbee6` (browser-verified `/dev/data-plot`); renderer per-chart shell + runtime gather branch `2b32bce` (**bundle-rendered-page-verified**: build histogram [2,3] + box [1,2,4,6,7] → "2/2 correct", zero console errors); editor Type picker + box TI-84 readout + tolerance field `19769c4` (`/playground`-verified: box + histogram preview recompute); dashboard per-variant formatting (`formatBins`/`formatFive`) `913744d`. Suite at ship: schema 244 / graph-kit 325 / renderer 516 / app 468 (1553); bundle base 37.5 / graphs 44.3 KiB (the widgets ride the lazy kit; only the +0.3 gather branch touched the inline bundle).

**▶ `data_plot` block family (statistics charts) — slice 1 (`display` + `build_dotplot`).** Kit `WXYPUC5W` on R2 (manifest committed `1744eb2`), `ingest-submission` v35 (wire 3–8, `verify_jwt:false`, MCP-verified) + `publish-activity` v86 live. The stats sibling of `interactive_graph`/`number_line`, reusing the same block→kit→pure-scorer template a third time. Design + 9 author-approved decisions in [design/data-plot-block.md](design/data-plot-block.md). Slice-1 scope: a **`display`** mode (dot plot / histogram / box plot as a static, ungraded stimulus — pure SVG the renderer draws, no kit/runtime, composes with a sibling question block) + a graded **`build_dotplot`** (the student stacks dots to reproduce a dataset's distribution). Built in 6 slices: (1) schema — `DataPlotBlock` + `DataPlotConfig` (reuses `NumberLineConfig` axis + histogram extras) + `DataPlotInteraction` union, `data: number[]` source (the answer is COMPUTED from it — decision 3a), wire **v7→v8** (`dataPlotResponses` map) + ingest accepts 3–8, `96e3da6`. (2) renderer — `data-plot-svg.ts` (static dot/histogram/box + `dotCounts`/`histogramBins`/`fiveNumberSummary` TI-84 exclusive-median) + block shell + print/no-JS SVG, `96e3da6`. (3) kit — pure `scoreDotplot` + a **dedicated SVG dot-plot board** (decision 6b, no JSXGraph — click/keyboard add/remove dots, tick snap) + `mountDataPlotQuestion`, `ee9a5bc` (browser-verified at `/dev/data-plot`). (4) runtime bridge — `dataPlotExt` seam mirroring `numberLineExt` + `attachDataPlotRuntime` in the kit + **`STORAGE_SCHEMA_VERSION` 8→9** + runtime wire v8 + `document.ts` variant scan (only a GRADED data_plot triggers the graphs bundle; display stays on base), `e2f8a39`. **BROWSER-VERIFIED on a bundle-rendered page** (inlined graphs runtime + lazy local kit): display box plot renders static, build→check→"1/1 correct"→reload-restores dots+score+verdict, zero console errors. (5) editor NodeView — `DataPlot` node + `DataPlotView` (a numeric **data-table** + a live static preview via `renderDataPlotSvg`; a Type picker spans build-dotplot / display dot·histogram·box) + serialize round-trip (replaces the omit-stub) + slash/Insert "Data plot", `39c26c6` (`/playground`-verified: live preview + mode switch recomputes the box plot). (6) dashboard — `dataPlots` index + `formatDotValues` + a "Data plot" Submissions table, `bd26f19`. **As-built deltas:** no author board (compute-from-data authoring makes drag-to-define moot); display is pure static SVG (no hydration). **▶ `number_line` block family (1-D graded) — all 6 slices.** Kit `WVG7FA6Y` (re-hashed from `E6G7VLZK`; entry + 7 chunks verified live on R2, manifest committed `eee04e4`), `ingest-submission` v34 (wire v7, `verify_jwt:false`, MCP-verified — the `--no-verify-jwt` flag survived) + `publish-activity` v85 live, deployed in the safe order. (6) **editor NodeView `5a551d6`** — `NumberLine` Tiptap node + `NumberLineView` reusing `mountNumberLineAuthor` (drag) + a numeric editor (point values / interval bounds + open-closed + unbounded=ray) + serialize round-trip (replaces the omit-stub) + slash-menu/Insert "Number line" entry + column-cell content expr + problemNumbering; browser-verified at `/playground`. **▶ number_line `plot_interval` shape buttons — FIXED (author-reported same day; branch `fix/number-line-shape-buttons`, merged to main `4ab2dba`).** The interval widget now offers the IDENTICAL shape controls as the graph ray/segment function — three pills **"Ray →" / "Ray ←" / "Segment"** plus contextual style pills (a shared `createIntervalShapeControls` in `packages/graph-kit/src/number-line-question.ts`, used by both `mountNumberLineQuestion` and `mountNumberLineAuthor`) replacing the old per-end closed/open/unbounded pills. Shape → board end-states: segment = both bounded; ray_positive = left bounded + right unbounded (→ +∞); ray_negative = mirror. A ray is one omitted bound, so the pure `scoreNumberLineInterval`, the stored interval, the board (draws the ray via the existing `unbounded` end-state), the wire, and storage are ALL unchanged — a control-surface swap only. Ray labels are static (a 1-D line has only two directions; no need for the graph's 8-way glyphs). Editor `NumberLineView` numeric panel got the same three shape pills (deriving shape from which bound is present), keeping the numeric bound inputs. BROWSER-VERIFIED: student `/dev/number-line` (shape pills, Ray → yields max-omitted payload, board draws ray, restore of a stored ray derives Ray → + scores correct, zero console errors) and editor `/playground` (board + numeric-panel pills in sync, Ray → collapses the JSON to a single bound). DEPLOYED kit-only: re-hashed `WXYPUC5W → graph-kit-CWAMAF2L.js` (manifest committed `95e3991`) + `publish-activity` redeployed (no ingest redeploy — no wire bump); existing number_line interval activities re-published.

**▶ Confidence/solution-reveal dedupe + confidence-radio locking fix (2026-07-11 → 12).** The four near-identical per-question-type confidence/solution-reveal loops were DEDUPED into one shared `renderSolutionAndConfidence` helper in `render.ts` (−0.5 KiB per variant; behavior-preserving, covered by the existing render/confidence jsdom tests) — `publish-activity` redeployed, author-confirmed. The dedupe surfaced a confidence-radio locking asymmetry, corrected across ALL FIVE question types — blank/MC (`render.ts`, `locked` now a required helper param) AND graph blocks (`graph-kit/src/runtime.ts`, same `section.locked` condition as the widget's own `setLocked`) now freeze their confidence radios in locked mode, matching what matching/ordering already did. Tests pin it (renderer 461 → 463, graph-kit 279 → 280). Shipped kit-first (`upload:graph-kit` → `deploy:publish`; the graph half re-hashed the kit → `graph-kit-P3IFHSRV.js`) — deployed + author-confirmed 2026-07-12.

**Eyeball clearances (2026-07-12, author-confirmed):** matching+ordering pointer-DRAG works on a real mouse/touch device; the drawable-row continuation-arrows checkbox works as intended (also FULLY verified at `/playground`: toggling "arrows" off flipped the checkbox, wrote `arrows: false` into the drawable's document model, and removed the arrowheads from the live preview — no longer pattern-only). The deferred sin-curve arrow bug that surfaced during that check was fixed same day (see STATE's Recently-completed / the sin-curve entry).

### Deploy clearances (2026-07-12)

All entries below shipped, deployed, and author-confirmed; durable per-feature status is STATE.md's Status-by-area table. Deploy clearances (the transient "Pending author actions" records) follow the narratives.

**▶ BOUNDED-CURVE graded interaction — unify ray/segment under "plot a bounded curve" (2026-07-11 later session; design pass → author greenlit plan 1–4 + eyes-open on the mechanic split → BUILT + harness-verified; DEPLOYED + author-confirmed 2026-07-11).** The audit surprise (again): a graded "bounded curve of any family" was ~90% already shipped as the dormant **`plot_function` + `domains[]`** path — student domain-endpoint handles (`getDomainXs`/`setDomainXs`), open/closed pills, `scoreDomainParts` folded into correctness/partial-credit, `resp.domain` (v4). Verified live in `/dev/graph-question` (two new `bounded-*` scenarios). **The one missing piece was clipping:** the student curve drew full-width (`functiongraph [fn]`) — the exact flaw that shelved it ("always looked infinite"). Fixed: the student curve now clips to the live endpoint x-positions (bold arc between them, faint dashed continuation beyond), mirroring the display path's `functiongraph [fn, lo, hi]`. **Endpoint UX reworked per author call** ("gliders aren't intuitive"): the amber diamond gliders are now **ray-style purple handles** — same look, same hollow(open)/filled(closed) rendering driven by the Start/End pills (`setDomainStyles`), so "drag the two ends + toggle open/closed" is the ray's mental model. **Unified authoring (family-based routing, exclusive → no double-scoring):** typing a domain clause into a "Plot a function" field makes a bounded curve — **linear/vertical + range → ray/segment** (the pills mechanic, via pure `linearDomainToRayOrSegment`, unit-tested), **curved family + range → `plot_function` + `domains[]`** (the endpoint-handle mechanic); no clause → unbounded curve. Both proven mechanics kept intact (no regression to shipped/published ray/segment). Editor-verified end to end: `y = x^2 - 4 for -2<=x<=3` → plot_function+domains (answer field round-trips the clause); `y = 2x+3 for 0<=x<=4` → plot_segment (picker flips to "Draw a ray or segment"). **Author board is WYSIWYG.** **THEN collapsed 5 handles → 3 (author question "can't the exterior points BE the endpoints?"):** the gliders are gone — the two OUTER curve handles (min-x / max-x) ARE the domain endpoints. A bounded parabola is now 3 handles (2 ends + 1 middle), an exp/log is 2 (both ends), exactly like a segment's two endpoints define its line; `deriveCurve` fits through all handles, the bold arc clips between the extreme handles, and those handles render hollow/filled via the pills (`applyBoundedStyles`, re-run on every move since the extreme identity can flip). Domain is DERIVED from the extreme handles (`getDomainXs` reads them; `resp.domain` still v4 — no wire change; restore rides `setPoints`, `setDomainXs` removed). Author endpoints are now draggable (no more pinned hack): a handle drag reports through `onChange` and the NodeView recomputes model + bound in ONE update (no clobber). `functionStartPoints` seeds the outer handles at the typed bounds + a GRID-ALIGNED middle (a raw 0.5 midpoint snapped off-curve and skewed the fit). Bugs fixed en route: `setDomainXs`→`[x,f(x)]` seeding drift; steep-curve handle CLAMP that fit a flatter curve (pre-existing). JXG-verified end to end: student `bounded-quad` = 3 handles, 0 gliders, domain (−2,3) from the extreme x's, `correct:true`, End-pill → the max handle goes hollow; author `y=x²−4 for −2≤x≤3` = 3 on-curve handles (exact fit), dragging the max endpoint x=3→4 updates `domains[0].max` to 4 while the model holds. **Kit + app only** → deploy is kit upload → publish-activity (no wire/ingest change — `resp.domain` already v4; no renderer-bundle drift). Suite: app 445 / graph-kit 279, all green; typecheck clean. (Same session earlier: graph answer fields render as MathLive with a math⇄text toggle, committed `5332cde`.)

**▶ CALCULATOR PARITY — Tier A batch (2026-07-11; design pass → all 9 author calls approved → BUILT + browser-verified; DEPLOYED + author-confirmed 2026-07-11).** The deliberate everything-the-current-engine-can-do sweep, permissive-by-default and gated by `CalculatorRestrictions` flags. **Kit:** new `solve.ts` (the solve-for-y core, side probes, `extractDomain`, `halfPlaneOutline` — extracted from formula.ts so evaluate.ts shares it cycle-free); `classifyExpression` gained `inequality` (ANY linear-in-y boundary — `y > sin(x)` works, author call 1 "spirit") + per-row `domain` kinds; inequality rows SOLVE AT PLOT TIME against the live slider scope (expression-list pass 3), so `y > a·x` tracks its slider; the board draws boundary (dashed=strict) + translucent row-color half-plane via ONE shared `halfPlaneOutline` (graded + display fills refactored onto it); per-row domains clip via NaN (no endpoint dots in the calculator, author call 6); the `for` clause also accepts Desmos-style `{x >= 0}` braces AND MathLive's spaced serialization (`f o r`). **Function table:** sec/csc/cot + inverses (trig gate + angle mode), sinh/cosh/tanh + inverses (trig gate, NEVER angle-converted — author call 3), floor/ceil/round(x,digits)/sign, nPr/nCr (overflow-safe), variadic min/max/mean; `mod` already worked via mathjs. **The real MathLive discovery:** unknown multi-letter names serialize as SPACED letters (`f l o o r(2.7)`, `10m o d 3`) — normalizeAsciiMath now reassembles the provided names (letter-boundary lookarounds, not \b: digits abut letters). **Keypad decision 7 REVERSED in verification:** the built-in numeric layout already ships `<`/`>` keycaps (shift ≤/≥) + variants on `=` — the planned graft was redundant and got dropped; gated-off keys just produce the friendly row message (physical-keyboard parity). **Schema (additive, no version bump):** `allowInequalities` default true; `RegressionModel` + 'logarithmic' end to end (schema enum/default, kit model list, drawer label `y = a + b·ln x`, dev harness) — stored docs carrying the explicit 3-model array stay 3-model (author-accepted, call 8). **Display curves:** the drawable-row formula field now ACCEPTS inequalities → shade + strict→dashed + domain (pure `curveFromFormula` in `drawableFormulaLogic.ts`, blankPopoverLogic pattern); a plain equation CLEARS shade (formula text = full statement of intent, call 4); `formatInequality` (formula.ts) round-trips the field display; importer inequality branch now carries domains (call 5); doc + Copy-AI prompt in lockstep (drift guard green). **Browser-verified** (`/dev/calculator` + `/playground` seed): 4-fill Desmos-style overlap screenshot (strict dashed, inclusive solid parabola, vertical shade-right, slider-driven), gate message on typed inequality, domain row clipping, log fit `y = 0.9999 + 2.00015 ln(x), r² = 1`, editor display graph shading + field round-trip `y > 2x + 1 for x >= 0`, zero console errors; print-SVG parity pinned by a composed dashed+shade+domain renderer test (graph-svg untouched — shade support pre-existed). **Dropped per author call 9:** movable points + Σ/Π notation (candidates, not scheduled). Suite: schema 208 / graph-kit 279 / renderer 461 / app 411 (**1359**); renderer bundle regenerated + committed.**

**▶ MATCHING + ORDERING question types (2026-07-10 sixth session; design → decided → BUILT + browser-verified → DEPLOYED, author-confirmed same day).** The last two ROADMAP Phase 2 question types, shipped together per [docs/design/matching-ordering-questions.md](docs/design/matching-ordering-questions.md) (the decided contract). **Schema:** `MatchingBlock` (items/targets/key; distractor targets; off-by-default `allowTargetReuse` = categorization-lite; item AND target figures reusing ChoiceImage/ChoiceGraph) + `OrderingBlock` (authored order = correct); wire **v5→v6** (`matches` map with per-pair earned/total + `orderings` map, all-or-nothing; ingest accepts v3–v6); `ActivityDocument` gained an explicit type annotation (the 14-member Block union outgrew tsc's declaration serializer, TS7056). **Renderer:** publish-time deterministic shuffle (seeded by block id, never the identity deal — an untouched ordering list must stay an omission), lettered target bank + dashed docks + ghost letters, textbook print conventions (write-the-letter lines / number-the-steps boxes; answer-key variant fills both), sr-status live regions. **Runtime (storage v7):** `matches.ts` + `orderings.ts` (mcs.ts shape) — POINTER DRAG over a keyboard select-then-place grammar (Enter lifts, arrows walk/move, Enter drops, Esc cancels, Delete un-docks; tap lifts/places/returns on touch); render() moves the REAL card into the dock (reuse mode: cloned chips, delegated-click removal) and re-sequences the order list from state; per-pair matching folds ITEM COUNT into section totals; drag transforms ride the popover-drag exception. **Editor:** Matching/Ordering NodeViews (mc-block chrome; key picker labels targets by TEXT because published letters follow the shuffle; incomplete-key warning; up/down reorder; shared figure panel extracted from MC), slash menu, columns content expr, serialize round-trip with key sanitizing (dangling refs dropped, duplicate targets collapsed sans reuse). **Import:** ```match fence (`item = option` split on the LAST ` = ` so equations keep their equals signs — a fix to the design doc's first-split spec; ` -> ` alternative; `\=` escape; leading `=` distractors; per-side images; `options: reuse`) + ```order fence (listed order = correct, `1.` markers stripped); doc + Copy-AI prompt + drift guard in lockstep. **Dashboard:** `matchings`/`orderings` index maps (parity guards extended via representativeBlock) + per-pair ✓/✗ and sequence-vs-key tables (content text, not letters). **Browser-verified on a bundle-rendered published page** (zero console errors): tap-to-pair docks card + ghost letter + SR narration, keyboard lift/cursor/place, keyboard reorder with focus continuity, check → "3 / 4 correct" (2 pairs + unpaired item + correct ordering), per-pair/per-position verdicts, solution reveal, reload → full restore, tap-undock. Pointer-DRAG specifics (transform follow, midpoint swaps) are pattern-verified only — synthetic pointers can't drive `setPointerCapture`; eyeball on a real mouse/touch device during the live-page pass. Suite: schema 206 / graph-kit 228 / renderer 460 / app 386 (**1280**). Commits `add2ac7` + the editor/app/ingest commit. **Deferred in-design:** per-pair feedback, ordering partial credit + interchangeable adjacents, SVG pair-lines, ordering item figures, categorization buckets (see the doc's deferred list).**

**▶ MC choice figures — per-choice images + static graphs (2026-07-10 fifth session; built + browser-verified; DEPLOYED + author-confirmed 2026-07-10 sixth session).** The additive widening the MC schema comment reserved at design time, landed exactly as reserved: optional `image {src, alt}` (URL-only, mirrors DefinitionImage/Phase-1 ImageBlock) and `graph {axis, drawables}` (reuses AxisConfig + display Drawables) on `MultipleChoiceOption` — **no schemaVersion/wire/storage bump; runtime untouched** (figures are presentation inside the choice label; scoring stays set-equality on ids). **Renderer:** figure emitted inside the `<label>` (clicking the figure selects the choice — live-verified), graph via the kit-free `renderGraphSvg` (same engine as the print/no-JS fallback → works on paper; `expression` drawables excluded by that engine's documented limit); **≥2 figured choices switch the fieldset to a 2-up grid** (`.mc-choices-grid`, the "which graph shows…" textbook layout; 1-col ≤40rem; letters pinned top-left via flex-start; revealed feedback spans the full row); `renderGraphSvg` now exported from the renderer index. **Editor:** 🖼 button per choice row (mirrors 💬) → figure panel: image URL/alt with validity+alt hints, or graph axis-window + drawables editor with a **live preview through the exact published-page engine**; `DisplayDrawableEditor` extracted from InteractiveGraphView into shared `editor/components/DrawableListEditor.tsx` (kinds prop; MC omits `expression`) — behavior-preserving, graph NodeView now consumes it. **Serialize:** figure fields validated with the real Zod schemas at the boundary (drop-malformed-keep-rest). **Import:** `![alt](url)` on a ```mc choice line becomes the choice image (image-only choices legal; bad URLs stay literal); per-choice graphs deliberately have NO fence syntax (editor-only) — doc + Copy-AI prompt updated in lockstep. **Browser-verified on a rendered published page** (bundle-rendered HTML, live runtime): grid + list layouts, figure-click selects, selections persist to storage for all three shapes (text/graph/image choices). Suite: schema 187 / graph-kit 228 / renderer 423 / app 361 (**1199**). Renderer bundle regenerated + committed.

**▶ Display-graph continuation arrows + domain/range semantics (2026-07-10 fourth session, second drop; author calls: arrows default-ON with per-drawable opt-out toggle; domain-only, NO y-range schema — YAGNI).** Static figures now speak the full textbook convention: **arrowhead = "keeps going", dot = "stops here."** Schema: additive optional `arrows` on curve/expression/ray drawables (old docs byte-identical). Kit: `display-arrows.ts` (pure, unit-tested) computes WINDOW-EXIT arrow placement — the subtlety is that most curves leave through the top/bottom (y = 2x + 3 exits a ±10 window at y = ±10), so arrows ride a sample-walk + rect-clip (with iterative refinement at asymptotes: a log curve's arrow lands at x = e⁻¹⁰ hugging the axis), never parked off-screen at the x-edges; `createDisplayBoard` draws them as short marker segments, suppressed per-end by an authored domain bound (dot territory) and entirely by `arrows: false`. Renderer print-SVG fallback got the parallel implementation (kit-free by design) so PAPER shows the same arrows. **Two real bugs found + fixed in passing:** (1) published-page `readDrawables` filtered kinds to a pre-Drop-5 list — `expression` and `ray` drawables SILENTLY VANISHED from published display figures (editor preview shared the same filter path); (2) the print-SVG ray parked its arrowhead marker at 3× the viewBox where the clipPath erased it — printed rays never showed their arrow. Editor: "arrows" checkbox on curve/formula/ray drawable rows (default on). Browser-verified via the dev harness's new "Static display" scenario (screenshot: line arrows at both exits, parabola dot + single arrow, segment dot-only, ray open-dot + exit arrow, arrows-off dashed line clean); the drawable-row checkbox itself is pattern-verified only (Insert dropdown resists synthetic events — same class as the standing drawable-editor-UI note in the Stage 5 entry). Suite: schema 187 / graph-kit 228 / renderer 416 / app 358 (**1189**). Renderer bundle regenerated + committed.

**▶ Graded function families — quadratic/exponential/logarithmic (2026-07-10 fourth session; design + build + browser-verified; DEPLOYED + author-confirmed 2026-07-11).** The audit surprise: the feature was ~80% already shipped as Drop 2 foundations (schema families, fit engine, scorer branches, `handlesForFamily`, formula-field family detection, import DSL, dashboard summaries — full table in [docs/design/graded-function-families.md](docs/design/graded-function-families.md)), so this was a **gap-closing pass**: no schema/wire/storage change. (1) **The one real bug — student handle seeding:** the board's generic y = 0 spread sits outside fitExponential (y > 0) / fitLogarithmic (x > 0) domains → handles but NO curve, silently. `startsForFamily` (graph-score.ts, beside handlesForFamily) seeds exp at rising positive-y grid lines / log at positive-x grid lines; wired via the plot_function recipe so `graph_inequality` boundaries inherit it. Mid-drag invalid points still hide the curve (JSXGraph NaN → display:none, verified) and the kit-runtime narrate line now appends "These points do not define a curve yet." (SR parity; RUNTIME.md §feedback updated). (2) **Editor:** picker option "Plot a line" → **"Plot a function"**; formula placeholder now shows an exponential example. Family switching stays formula-driven (author call 1a). (3) **Quadratic built-in classifiers** (author call 3c — exp/log wait on observed data): opens-wrong-way (flipped `a`, guarded against near-degenerate keys), y-axis shift, mirrored-vertex, width nudges. (4) **Dashboard:** plot_function rows now show the student's points re-fit into an equation ("≈ y = 1x² + 0x + -4") via `fitStudentEquation` + shared `formatFunctionModel` (`GraphInfo.functionFamily` carries the family). (5) **`/dev/graph-question` grew a scenario picker** (all graded families + a quadratic inequality) — the browser-verify surface: exp/log seed-and-score, 3-handle parabola, and **y > x² − 4 end-to-end (dashed boundary, above-shading, correct: true) — quadratic inequalities worked with zero additional code, as the schema promised.** Suite: schema 186 / graph-kit 216 / renderer 413 / app 358 (**1173**); typecheck/lint green. Kit + app changes only → deploy is kit upload → publish-activity → app.

**▶ Graph-runtime-into-kit bundle-budget move + MathLive entry split (2026-07-10 third session — the "lazy graph fix"; decided prior session; author-approved design incl. both riders).** `runtime/graph-integration.ts` is now a THIN BRIDGE (slim init walk, state seed, checkpoint score fold, submit gather — everything that must survive a kit-load failure, so restored answers still score/submit kit-less); the DOM-heavy plumbing (attribute parsing, block chrome render, widget mount/restore/lock, display figures) moved to **`graph-kit/src/runtime.ts`** behind `attachGraphRuntime(ctx)` — one attach covers graded + display blocks (the `wireGraphDisplays` seam method merged into `wireGraphs`). **Shared contract types** live in `graph-kit/src/runtime-contract.ts` (`GraphBlockState` moved there; state.ts re-exports), imported by the bridge with `import type` via a new `@activity/graph-kit/runtime-contract` export — zero bytes, drift = compile error (renderer gained a type-only workspace devDep). **Sizes:** graphs variant 27.8→**23.8 KiB** (base 21.2 unchanged); future graph runtime features grow the cached kit, not the inline bundles. **Rider shipped:** `mountCalculator` on the kit entry is now an async wrapper dynamic-importing calculator.ts, so **MathLive is its own chunk** — kit entry 34.4 KiB min/11.4 gz + static mathjs chunk (41.3 gz); graph-only pages never fetch MathLive (network-verified); calculator pays +1 RTT on first open; both app call sites (config-drawer preview, /dev/calculator) updated to the awaited handle with a cancelled-effect guard. **Verified:** typecheck/lint green, 1158 tests (kit chrome tests moved to `graph-kit/tests/runtime.test.ts`; runtime-variant marker is now `attachGraphRuntime`), plus a browser pass on a REAL published page (renderer-bundle HTML + built kit served same-origin): board mounts through the bridge, keyboard plot → SR narration, check → verdict/solution/1-1 score, reload → restore, display graph draws, no MathLive fetch. Accepted trade: graph block chrome (restored verdicts/solutions) paints when the kit lands, a beat after first paint — same timing the board always had. **DEPLOYED + author-confirmed live 2026-07-10** (kit + publish-activity + app; cleared above). Committed `99129ae`.

**▶ Numeric answers + MULTIPLE CHOICE (2026-07-10 session — the "question types beyond blanks" build; author scope call: standalone question type, NOT a graph-block field; committed `1a997fd` + `c334b96` + `dce312a` + `bb96f7d` + `7a8584d`; DEPLOYED + author-confirmed live 2026-07-10, cleared above).** (1) **Numeric blanks** — `BlankToken.answerType: 'numeric'` + optional `tolerance` (additive/optional; old docs byte-identical) ride the EXISTING `data-blank-strategy` seam: the runtime's new `numeric` strategy parses both sides (decimals, fractions, mixed numbers, sci notation, commas, leading `$`) and compares within tolerance (+1e-9 epsilon), so 0.5 = 1/2 = .50; non-numeric key entries fall back to exact match; grouped blanks inherit it through `evaluateAnswer`. Popover "Numeric answer" checkbox + tolerance field (pure `resolveToleranceCommit`), green `#` chip cue, `{{=12}}` / `{{=3.14 +- 0.01}}` / `{{~=3}}` import syntax. NO wire change. (2) **`multiple_choice` block** — prompt + 2+ rich-inline choices (text + math; images/static-graphs widen later via ADDITIVE fields on MultipleChoiceOption, decided at design), single-select radios or `multiSelect` checkboxes, scored **all-or-nothing** set equality; per-choice `feedback` (the MC mistakeFeedback analogue, revealed post-check for selected choices); block fields mirror fill_in_blank (solution/confidence/skills/workSpace). Wire **v4→v5** (`choices` map, `ChoiceResponse`; ingest accepts v3/v4/v5), `STORAGE_SCHEMA_VERSION` **5→6** (blob gains `mcs`), base runtime 21.2 KiB (over the 20 KiB soft target — core question machinery; lever unchanged). Runtime: `mcs.ts` + McRef/McBlockState; render owns selection sync (restore is state-only), verdict classes + aria-invalid on SELECTED choices only (no answer leak), locked freeze; deliberately NO immediate-feedback mode (closed-form brute-forcing). Editor: NodeView with in-place choice list (correct markers w/ radio semantics, feedback disclosures, remove gated at 2), zero-correct warning, shared `problemNumberAt` (fixes editor-number drift across the three question views). Import: **```mc fence** (`( )`/`(x)`, square brackets or >1 correct → multi-select, `:: feedback`, `solution:`, `options: confidence`) — doc + Copy-AI prompt in lockstep, drift guard extended. Dashboard: `index.mcs` + "Multiple choice" table (lettered choices, answer summary). Browser-verified at `/playground` (both features); runtime jsdom-tested on the markup contract; live published-page pass CLEARED 2026-07-10 (second pass). **Also swept into `c334b96`:** the author's static graph-paper SVG fallback (was untracked-but-imported → clean checkouts/CI would have failed; now committed with a fresh bundle). RUNTIME.md contract updated (numeric strategy, MC block section, stale version constants fixed). **Next up:** matching + ordering BUILT same session (see the entry above). Per-choice partial credit flag DEFERRED indefinitely (author call 2026-07-10: low feature value). Runtime budget call recorded in the design doc: accept base growth (measured comparables: Desmos calc.js ~1.0 MB transferred, DeltaMath landing ~360 KB — the 20 KiB soft target is self-discipline, not market parity; 40 KiB ceiling is the line).

## 2026-07-08/09 — graph session, reference pop-up, typography, toolbar (moved from STATE 2026-07-10)

**▶ Ray/segment SHAPE TOGGLES (2026-07-09 second pass, author redirect after testing Drop C — the pre-drawn ray leaked the answer's shape/direction).** The shape is now the STUDENT'S choice and a graded part: three identical pills on every ray/segment question (Ray → / Ray ← / Segment; glyphs + segment style labels re-orient live for vertical lines), a faint dashed NEUTRAL line until a shape is picked (unpicked = unanswered part, inequality-side precedent), and the drawn figure always mirrors the answer state — arrowhead appears/flips with the choice (recomputed mid-drag when handle order flips), endpoint dots hollow/fill with open/closed toggles ("the picture IS the answer"; deliberately agent-read-in-friendly). Scoring: ray = shape + placement (endpoint hit + collinear, shape-agnostic) + style (3 parts); segment = shape + 2 positions + 2 styles (5 parts; styles earnable only when a segment is drawn). Board gained a dynamic `linearShape` mode (`setShape`); author board keeps the fixed flags. Responses canonicalize points lesser-first + carry optional `shape` (additive in v4). Classifiers + authored matchers reworked (matchers accept ray OR segment strings on either type — the anticipated mistake is often the other figure). Suite 1009; browser-verified end-to-end incl. restore. Commit `8763898`; DEPLOYED 2026-07-09 (live passes = action #2). Same-day follow-ups: teacher-side authoring uses the SAME pills (one "Draw a ray or segment" picker entry, shared kit helper, toggling converts plot_ray ↔ plot_segment, `47c3d1d`) and TRUE-direction glyph fix (8-way arrows computed from the drawn line — a steep negative-slope ray reads "Ray ↓", never a lying "Ray ↑"; freeform answer applies remount the author board via a formulaEpoch so handles/pills jump to the typed answer, `9dd1278`).

**▶ Graph feedback + ray/segment session (2026-07-09 first pass, author live-testing feedback → three drops, all committed + browser-verified; deployed by author same day).** (A) **Leak fixes:** the aria-live position narration was VISIBLE (handed students their coordinates — fatal for plot-the-point); it now carries `data-mode="narrate"` and is SR-only, while post-check results (`data-mode="result"`) stay visible. Inequality pills dropped their `(≤ ≥)`/`(< >)` legends. The markdown DSL accepts `dotted` as a `dashed` synonym (an unknown style token silently downgraded the drawable, losing style + shade — the one reproducible vector for author issue #4). (B) **Mistake feedback:** built-in classifiers (kit `mistakes.ts`, pure; default ON, per-block off switch): plot_point swapped/sign-flipped coordinates; linear slope/intercept traded/sign/one-off nudges; inequality per-part nudges riding the existing parts scoring; ray/segment part nudges — messages never teach the convention being assessed (test-pinned). Authored anticipated mistakes: `mistakeFeedback[{match, feedback}]` on the block (match = the same freeform syntax as the answer field, parsed kit-side, scored with the block's tolerances; first match beats built-ins; rich feedback pre-rendered into `js-graph-mistake-content` templates the runtime clones). Editor: Advanced settings → "Mistake feedback" section with live match validation. DSL: `mistake: <wrong> :: <feedback>` lines + `no-builtin-feedback` option. Additive schema/attrs/state — NO schemaVersion/wire/storage bumps. (C) **plot_ray + plot_segment** replace the domain-glider approach (which never clipped the drawn line — a student's "ray" always looked infinite): student drags two handles, the board draws a REAL ray (arrowhead, `straightFirst:false`) or segment, endpoint pills toggle open/closed and the handles render hollow/filled to match. Parts scorers (ray: position+direction+style /3; segment: order-independent endpoints with styles traveling /4), partial-credit-ready. Wire: `RayResponse`/`SegmentResponse` join `GraphResponseV4` (v4-only members — **ingest redeploy required before publishing one**). Freeform `ray (1, 2) through (3, 4) open` / `segment (1, 2) to (3, 4)` (parser + round-trip formatters); editor picker "Draw a ray"/"Draw a segment"; DSL `answer: ray …`; dashboard shows endpoint styles. `for x >= 0` authoring now steers to rays/segments (glider student path kept for already-published pages). **Drive-by fix of a latent restore bug:** `restore()` re-scored via `setPoints`' onChange BEFORE extras applied — a reloaded answer re-scored with stale style/strict/side and overwrote the stored result; extras now apply first (affected inequalities too). Suite 1004; browser-verified end-to-end incl. reload.

**▶ Reference panel as a pop-up SHIPPED (2026-07-08, author-requested same day; browser-verified headless on a rendered published page).** The published-page reference panel is now a calculator-style floating window: `renderReferenceTool` emits a `.reference-summon` button + hidden `.reference-float` panel (server-rendered content — NO kit involvement, decided per design pass: window chrome isn't worth hundreds of KiB and panels must work with no kit URL); both tools' buttons live in ONE fixed bottom-right **`.tool-corner` cluster** (calculator's `.calculator-tool` is now static inside it). The rewritten ~1.3 KiB sidecar does summon/close (button hides while open; focus panel→button; Escape respects `defaultPrevented`) + calculator-clamp header drag; resize is native CSS `resize:both`; geometry memory is FREE (inline styles + never-destroyed element). Panel anchors bottom-LEFT (open calculator sits bottom-right); phone = viewport clamp (`max-width: calc(100vw - 2rem)`, verified 343px @ 375). Old scroll-clearance ResizeObserver + `has-reference-panel` padding deleted; `renderReferenceBox`/`printReferencePanel` print box untouched (PDF-verified: box prints, cluster hidden). One real bug caught in browser verify: `display:flex` on the panel class silently defeated the `hidden` attribute — restated `.reference-float[hidden]{display:none}`. No-JS behavior change accepted (panel stays hidden; print box is the JS-free surface). RUNTIME.md + DECISIONS.md updated; 8-case jsdom sidecar suite added (definitions-test pattern); render tests updated to the new markup (sidecar-inlining asserts compare against the committed generated bundle itself). Deployed 2026-07-09; live pass = action #2 (re-publish panel activities).

**▶ Activity-wide typography BUILT + DEPLOYED (2026-07-08; browser-verified at `/playground` + `/dev/config-drawer` + rendered published pages; fonts uploaded, publish-activity + app deployed same day — live-page font verify is pending action #2).** All locked decisions as specced: additive optional **`meta.typography { font, fontSize }`** (no schemaVersion bump — old docs parse unchanged; the drawer omits the field while both controls sit at the defaults, so untouched docs stay structurally identical). **Font menu v1 (all SIL OFL):** Default (system stack), Lexend, Atkinson Hyperlegible, Andika, Comic Neue — the CSS specifics live renderer-side in `packages/renderer/src/typography.ts` (`FONT_REGISTRY`/`FONT_MENU`/`fontFamilyValue`/`fontFaceCss`/`typographyStyleTag`); the schema only constrains the menu, so a new font is one registry row + one upload. **The CSS-var seam:** a per-document `<style>` sets `--activity-font-family` on `:root` (read on `body`, so the hint popover/calculator outside `<main>` match) + `--activity-font-size` (read on `.activity-container`); headings/section titles converted rem→em so they scale off the ACTIVE base — screen base on screen, `--print-font-size` in print (which keeps sole ownership of print body sizing via the unchanged `@media print` override; em headings now track it too, an improvement). **Self-hosted on R2:** `pnpm build:fonts` uploads 15 WOFF2s (~260 KiB, from pinned `@fontsource/*` packages — same bytes the editor loads through Vite, so authoring is WYSIWYG offline with no Google CDN anywhere); `publish-activity` passes `fontsBaseUrl` (from `FONTS_R2_PREFIX`, exported via the renderer bundle so the layout can't drift). Print view passes the same base to `renderActivityForPrint`; the **foldable threads one typography tag through BOTH its measure iframe and final document** (family changes text metrics — measuring in the fallback but printing in the real font would mis-paginate; measure already awaits `fonts.ready`). Config drawer **Settings** gained Font + Base text size (12–24 px, schema-clamped) + a live preview line; `/playground` gained a dev picker driving the editor canvas. **Per-span `textStyle` mark stays PARKED but slotted for:** `fontFaceCss` takes a LIST of families, and span-level inline styles win the cascade over the activity-wide vars — the mark layer adds without reworking this one. Tests: schema typography suite (9), renderer typography suite (13), drawer typography cases (3).

**▶ Editor toolbar reorganization SHIPPED (2026-07-08, browser-verified at `/playground`).** The approved three-tier design, as specced: (1) the flat toolbar keeps ONLY selection formatting (B, I, U, code, sub/sup, Define, ƒx inline math); (2) ALL block insertion moved into one keyboard-accessible **"+ Insert" dropdown** (`editor/components/InsertMenu.tsx`) **driven by `slashMenuItems.ts`** — items gained `group` headings (Text/Math/Structure/Questions), `range` became optional (`begin()` skips the slash-range delete on the toolbar path), `referenceSafe` scopes the reference-panel variant, and a contextual **"Answer blank"** item (gated `isEnabled: isActive('fillInBlank')`, disabled-with-hint in the dropdown, hidden in the slash menu) absorbed the old Blank button incl. its rAF node-selection; the slash menu and dropdown can no longer drift — a future block type is one entry in one file (README checklist updated to say so); (3) **column controls are contextual** — Grid/+Col/−Col/Width/Cell height render only when the selection is inside a `columns` block (+/− still use `editor.can()` at the 2–6 bounds). Dropdown keyboard access: trigger opens on click/ArrowDown, arrows cycle enabled items (disabled skipped), Home/End, Enter picks, Escape closes + returns focus to the trigger, outside click closes. Removed the slash menu's dead **"Quote"** item — blockquote is disabled in both editors' StarterKit config, so its `toggleBlockquote()` command has been a throw since Stage 9 (flag if quotes are ever wanted: re-enable the extension + re-add the item). Verified live: every block type inserted from the dropdown, Answer blank gate both ways, column cluster appears/disappears with selection, slash menu open/filter/insert + in-problem `/blank`. Guard test `slashMenuItems.test.ts` (8 cases).

**Same-day follow-up pass (author-approved decisions): text-style picker + header/drawer overhaul, both browser-verified.** (a) **TextStylePicker** — headings/paragraph/lists are block TRANSFORMS, not insertions, so they moved out of "+ Insert" into a Docs-style current-style dropdown at the toolbar's left (trigger shows the live block style via new per-item `isActive` predicates on the Text group; same keyboard contract as InsertMenu; both editor variants; slash `/h1` etc. unchanged — still one item list, now three surfaces). (b) **Activity-editor header + config drawer** — the four stacked disclosure strips (⚙ Activity settings, 🖨 Print layout, 📎 Reference panel, 🧮 Calculator) are now icon+label chips in ONE merged header (with Print view/Submissions/Import restyled to match; Publish keeps primary styling) opening a **right-side drawer** (`components/ActivityConfigDrawer.tsx`), one section at a time; state cues: emerald dot = calculator enabled / reference has content, amber dot = locked-mode warning (the inline warning banner STAYS on the page — never hidden behind a button). Drawer bodies stay MOUNTED while hidden (the reference panel's embedded editor must fire onCreate once and keep edits; same for the calculator preview) — invariant documented in the file header + pinned by `ActivityConfigDrawer.test.tsx` (mount/section-switch/dots; RTL `cleanup` required — jsdom resolves scoped `#id` queries document-first with duplicate ids). Escape-in-drawer closes only when `!e.defaultPrevented` so popover/slash Escapes inside the panel editor don't double-close; focus returns to the opener chip. New dev bench **`/dev/config-drawer`** (header cluster + drawer with local state — ActivityEditor itself sits behind auth, so this is the browser-verifiable surface; nav chips are visual-parity dummies). **Third-pass fix (browser-verified):** the calculator preview now mounts FLOATING (the published-page mode) instead of inline — the 30rem graphing widget never fit the 26rem drawer; a drawer toggle button summons/hides the real draggable panel over the editor (kit `onToggle` syncs ×/Esc; Esc closes only the panel; section-switch/drawer-close hides it with the mounted-while-hidden body). **Drive-by fix of a broken-on-main bug:** the Stage 5 graphs-in-columns drop added `interactiveGraph` to the column cell's content expression, which crashed `ReferencePanelEditor` (extension not registered) — i.e. the whole `/activity/:id` route rendered blank since `7d96092`. Fixed by registering `InteractiveGraph` there (same registered-only-for-the-schema pattern as FillInBlank/Blank, commented); the new drawer test's mount line guards the class of bug.

## 2026-07-08 and earlier — long-running STATE narratives: graphing build-out (Drops 0–7), Phase 2.7 stages, Stage 5 graph block, vocabulary definitions (moved from STATE 2026-07-12 drift-audit)

These are the standing "Current focus" narratives that lived in STATE.md through the graphing arc; every feature below is shipped, deployed, and author-verified (durable status: STATE's Status-by-area table; decisions: DECISIONS.md + the design docs).

**▶ 2026-07-08 session — the graphing feature build-out (Drops 0–7), all committed-ready, 902 tests green.** (0) Runtime split into TWO build variants behind the `graphExt` seam (`runtime/graph-integration.ts` + `.noop.ts`; base 17.0 KiB — under target; graphs variant ~22 KiB inlined only on graph pages; document.ts picks by body scan; CI guard covers both bundles). (1) Graphs fit in columns (ColumnCellBlock + editor content expr + screen-only min-width floor with overflow-x scroll; browser-verified 2/3-col). (2) Schema foundations: FunctionModel = linear/quadratic/exponential/logarithmic/vertical (+ `fitLogarithmic`; params match the fitters); plot_function→`models[]`, shade_region→`regions[]` (arrays-of-one, systems additive); `partialCredit`/`allowNoSolution`/`noSolutionCorrect` flags; `EndpointStyle`; partial scorers (`score*Partial` → {earned,total}). (3) **Freeform command line**: `packages/graph-kit/src/formula.ts` `parseGraphFormula` — ANY equation format (slope-intercept/standard/point-slope/bare expr/`x=k`), inequalities (sign→strict+side, incl. vertical left/right), point lists, `for x >= 0` domain clauses; numeric solve-for-y + sample-and-fit family detection; `formatModel` round-trips; editor `FormulaField` two-way synced with handles (browser-verified: standard form, quadratic, vertical incl. board line-through-handles, error steering to Display). (4) **Inequalities**: `graph_inequality` interaction (boundary=FunctionModel so quadratic/vertical work; strict; shadeSide above/below/left/right; array-of-one); kit widget control bar (solid/dotted pills, shade-side pills + click-side-on-board, half-plane shading via hand-built filled curve); **wire v3→v4** (InequalityResponse + noSolution + earned/total; CheckpointResult.score now fractional; ingest accepts v3 AND v4, migrates on write; runtime STORAGE_SCHEMA_VERSION 4→5); partial credit + no-solution flow end-to-end (runtime fractional section scores + payload); editor picker + typed-inequality authoring (browser-verified). (5) **Display upgrades**: drawables gained curve style/shade/domain, `expression` (plots ANY formula by sampling), `ray`, segment `endpoints`, point open/closed — display board renders all. (6) **Rays/segments**: `FunctionInteraction.domains[]` (parallel to models) filled by the `for …` clause + drawn endpoints on display; the STUDENT endpoint-drag widget UX was the flagged follow-up (scoring stayed on curve params until then). (7) **Markdown ```graph DSL** (docs/markdown-import-format.md): axes/prompt/answer/show/options lines through the same parser; importer + 6 tests; pdf-import.md + photo-grading.md graph sections. **Follow-ups (same-day second pass): ALL BUILT** — the Copy-AI prompt teaches the ```graph fence (doc re-embedded, drift guard green); the drawable-list editor edits every kind (+ formula/ray rows, open/closed + dashed toggles); the narrow-column advisory warns on graphs in 3+-column layouts; and the **endpoint-drag widget landed**: domain-restricted plot_function questions grow amber GLIDER handles riding the curve (drag to place the ray/segment ends) + Start/End open-closed pills, scored per part (`scoreDomainParts`, position ±0.25 + style) and folded into correctness/partial credit; response carries an optional `domain` object (additive within v4); restore/lock wired. Author verify CLEARED 2026-07-10 (fourth session). (The glider approach was later superseded by first-class plot_ray/plot_segment and the 2026-07-11 bounded-curve rework — see those entries.)

**▶ Phase 2.7 — Graphing track. Stages 1–4 + full graphing-calculator UX pass, all browser-verified.** The **`@activity/graph-kit`** package is the kit, consumed two ways from one impl (published pages lazy-`import()` it; the editor preview imports it). Stage 1 (scientific) + Stage 2 (graphing, JSXGraph lazy-split); **Stage 3** (`2bc3f68`+`9df8738`) — the Texas Algebra I regression flow (Data view: table → linear/quad/exp → equation + r², TI-84 conventions, `allowedRegressionModels` flag); **Stage 4** (`f643087`) — the Desmos-defining **expression list** (color-coded MathLive rows: curves, `(a,b)` points, `a=3` sliders with live-drag re-plot, `maxExpressions` cap). **Then a large author-driven UX overhaul (see the 2026-07-06 entry below):** click-drag pan + on-graph zoom/reset/fit buttons + drag-resizable **floating draggable panel**; MathLive **virtual keyboard rendered inside the panel** (custom button pad dropped in graphing; single layout, ☰ menu hidden) with a clear toggle button + **responsive keyboard scaling**; list/board **splitter**; **hover-trace** `(x,y)` readout; bare-number rows show `= value`; remembered size/position/split; full-width click target; **sticky axes** (scale stays visible when an axis pans off-screen). Comma-key gap CLOSED (`dd5466f`, browser-verified): the graphing keyboard grafts a comma keycap onto MathLive's numeric layout after the decimal separator, so touch-only students can type a point `(a,b)`. Spreadsheet-paste into the Data table was **dropped** (author call 2026-07-06; revisit later if wanted). Kit uploaded + live as of the 2026-07-08 full deploy pass.

**▶ Stage 5 — graded interactive-graph block: SLICE 1 (2.7a plot-a-point) through display mode.** The design doc ([design/interactive-graph-block.md](design/interactive-graph-block.md)) predated the kit; the reconciled shape: the graded widget rides the SAME lazy graph kit as the calculator (not a separate `graph-widget.js`), and the runtime is inlined (no per-publish `runtime.js`). Four coherent commits: **schema** (`db70beb`) — `InteractiveGraphBlock` + `AxisConfig` + a `plot_point` discriminated union, `graphResponses` submission map at a **v2→v3** bump (`PointResponse`), factory + Block-union registration; **renderer** (`db70beb`) — the block shell (prompt, focusable `role=application` canvas, aria-live feedback) + additive data-attrs (`data-graph-config`/`-answer-key`/`-kit-src`, threaded from `calculatorKitUrl`, omitted on print) + screen/print CSS; **kit widget** (`f949c33`) — `board.ts` `createPointAnswerBoard` (fixed-window, snap-to-grid draggable point, arrow-key nudge + Shift-fine, clamped) + pure `isPointCorrect` scorer + `mountGraphQuestion` entry (dynamic-imports the board so JSXGraph stays lazy) — **browser-verified at `/dev/graph-question`** (render, keyboard nav, position narration, tolerance scoring, lock, restore; fixed a real bug where `JSXGraph.initBoard` clobbers the container's `role`/`aria-label`); **runtime** (`d3fd35a`) — init walks the block into a `GraphRef`, the `graphs.ts` sidecar `import()`s the kit + mounts + bridges moves into `state.graphs`, checkpoints score each graph as one unit (unanswered = omission), submit gathers `graphResponses` + folds it into the score (payload v3), `STORAGE_SCHEMA_VERSION 3→4` persists graphs, render owns the block chrome (feedback/result/solution/confidence/lock), `ingest-submission` enforces v3. **Slice-1 follow-ups:** N-handle multi-point — one draggable handle per correct point, Tab cycles them, scored consume-once ("plot ALL," like blank groups); replaced the ambiguous either-root simplification (`75ddbc9`, browser-verified 2-handle). Dashboard renders `graphResponses` — a "Graph questions" table showing plotted point(s) vs the answer key (`4364039`). **Slice 2 — editor NodeView (`f7d3dd8`):** authorable in the real builder (slash-menu "Interactive graph"). Built B-shaped — an interaction picker + progressive-disclosure "Advanced settings" so `plot_line`/`shade_region` slot in additively. `mountGraphAuthor` reuses the SAME kit board in author mode (the teacher drags handles → `correctPoints`, "what the teacher sees is what the student gets"); `InteractiveGraph` Tiptap node with an editable-prompt `NodeViewContent` + structured attrs; `serialize.ts` round-trips both directions. Browser-verified at `/playground`; serialize round-trip + schema-valid output unit-tested. Fixed a nested-focusable a11y bug (host + inner board both had `role=application`). **Coordinate-system taxonomy decided:** number lines (1D, K-8) and stats charts (histogram/box/dot) became SEPARATE block families (`number_line`, `data_plot`) reusing this same block→kit→pure-scorer template — NOT forced under `interactive_graph`'s 2D AxisConfig (both since shipped, 2026-07-12). **2.7b plot-a-line (`1768087`):** a `plot_function` interaction — the student places two points and the widget fits + draws the line through them, scored on slope/intercept (per-parameter tolerances). Fully additive; future-proofed for quad/exp/log around a `family` discriminated union + the EXISTING regression fit engine, so growing 2 points (line) → 3 (parabola) is one schema member + one `fitX` call (the graded families landed `38b9fbe` 2026-07-10). **2.7c shade-region (`bc75b72`):** the student drags a polygon's vertices to cover a target region (shaded live); scored by area overlap — **intersection-over-union ≥ minOverlap** — via `pointInPolygon` (ray cast) + `polygonOverlap` (100×100 grid sampling, no clipping dependency, pure + testable). **Graph-as-stimulus — DISPLAY mode:** a `display` member of the `GraphInteraction` union carrying `drawables: Drawable[]` (discriminated on `kind`: `point`, `curve` [reuses `FunctionModel`], `segment`, `polygon`; `label` deferred/YAGNI). Additive → no schemaVersion bump, no `GraphResponse` variant. Renders a **read-only figure** via `createDisplayBoard`/`mountGraphDisplay` (JSXGraph, no handles/drag/pan, `role="img"`); the renderer emits it as `data-block-category="content"` with `data-graph-drawables`, pulls no problem number, and the runtime mounts it kept OUT of `graphs`/`graphBlockIds` (never scored, never in the submit payload). Editor: Type picker "Display (static graph)" + live read-only preview + per-drawable numeric list editor; slash-menu "Static graph" insert. Decided (per author): display is a **mode of `interactive_graph`**, not a separate block family. (The "answer surface as a graph-block field" seam sketched here was RETIRED 2026-07-10, author call — graph + typed/MC answer composes as a display graph + a sibling question block instead.) The editor preview board + per-drawable list editor UI were browser-verified 2026-07-12 at `/playground`. Remaining browser-UNVERIFIED surface: the deployed R2 display path only (same class as the graded R2 path).

**▶ Phase 2 — Inline vocabulary definitions (SHIPPED 2026-06-19; `publish-activity` redeployed 2026-07-06 — reaches a page on its next re-publish).** Teacher selects text → "Define" → types a definition; the published page shows it in a popover. Four slices: **schema/renderer** (`2329580`) — marks moved from a string enum to an object discriminated union (a `z.preprocess` upgrades legacy string marks on read, so stored activities keep parsing with no `schemaVersion` bump), `DefinitionMark { definition, glossaryKey? }`, renderer emits the escaped `<span class="definition" data-definition…>` (reserved `data-glossary-key` only when set), serialize round-trips both ways; **runtime** (`e26dea7`) — a self-contained ~1.7 KiB `definitions.ts` sidecar (mirrors the reference-panel sidecar; inlined only when a page has a definition) opens a popover on click/tap/Enter/Space, Esc/outside/scroll closes, focus returns; **editor** (`16e0470`) — `Definition` mark + "Define" toolbar button + a single-host `DefinitionPopoverHost`/`DefinitionEditPopover` (mark-based via `getMarkRange`; commit-on-close, a blank draft removes the mark). Browser-verified on `/playground`: apply→popover→type→commit, re-open prefilled, Remove keeps the word, dotted-underline cue (computed-style confirmed). **Rich-content upgrade (`dc55271`):** definition content went from a plain string to formatted text + inline math + one optional image — the popover uses the shared `InlineRichTextEditor` (the hint control) + a compact image control (URL paste, or upload via `upload-image` when an activityId is present); the renderer pre-renders the content into a hidden `<template class="js-definition-content">` the sidecar clones into the popover (`data-definition` kept as a plain-text fallback); the schema's content type forbids nested definitions and a `z.preprocess` upgrades the legacy string. Browser-verified: rich editor + image control, URL→preview→commit, image-only definition, re-open prefills content + image. Reasoning in [design/vocabulary-definitions.md](design/vocabulary-definitions.md). **Phase 4 (future):** the account/tenant-scoped glossary store + implicit-reuse + auto-suggest (same doc).

## 2026-07-06 — Calculator graphing UX overhaul (author-driven, browser-verified)

A run of author feedback turned the graphing calculator from functional-but-rough into a genuinely Desmos-like tool. Every item below was verified live at `/dev/calculator` (agent-driven, once the author's dev server freed port 5173). Commits, oldest→newest: floating panel `18025fc`, graphing overhaul `968f629`, keyboard button + scaling `d51d334`, full-width click fix `371234b`, sticky axes `47d57be`.

- **Floating, draggable panel (`18025fc`).** The published-page calculator opened in-flow at the fixed bottom-right corner and grew up over the work with no way to move it. Now the panel is a floating window (`position: fixed`) draggable by its header (clamped so the header can't be lost off-screen; header buttons still click). Gated on a `hooks.floating` flag so the inline **editor preview stays in-flow** (a fixed preview would jump to a screen corner). The summon button hides while the panel is open (they shared the corner). Expression field made taller.
- **Graphing overhaul (`968f629`).** Bare no-variable rows (`2+3`, `sin(30)`) now show `= value` as a **calculation** instead of a flat line (new `'calculation'` `ExpressionRow` kind; `y=5` still plots). The **custom button pad is gone in graphing mode** — input is the MathLive field + its **virtual keyboard rendered inside the panel** (`mathVirtualKeyboard.container = panel`, single `'numeric'` layout so no matrix/greek switcher). A **draggable splitter** rebalances the list vs the board. Extras (all author-greenlit): **hover-trace** readout of `(x, y)` on the nearest curve; **zoom-to-fit** board button; panel size/position/split **remembered across re-opens within the session** (module-level, not storage — calculator persistence stays deferred). Gotcha found live: setting MathLive `menuItems` throws "not mounted" on a fresh field → hid the ☰ menu (and the built-in in-field keyboard toggle) via CSS `::part(...)` + `!important` instead.
- **Keyboard button + responsive scaling (`d51d334`).** The ⌨ toggle read as a bare glyph → now a bordered button with hover + pressed state (`aria-pressed` synced across rows) and a tooltip. The keyboard crammed when the panel was small → the graphing panel is a **CSS size-query container**, so the in-panel keyboard + field scale down at 27rem/23rem breakpoints via MathLive's `--keycap-*` variables; bonus, `container-type` also activates MathLive's own `clamp(16px,4cqw,24px)` keycap-font default. (Class names + variables read from MathLive 0.109 source.)
- **Full-width click target (`371234b`).** Switching rows felt clunky — clicks only registered near the caret. Root cause was self-inflicted: `display: flex` on the `math-field` (added to center tall math) collapsed MathLive's editable area to the content width. Removed it (MathLive centers content itself) + added row-level click-to-focus for the dot/gaps. Verified: clicking the field's empty right edge now focuses it; fractions still render centered.
- **Sticky axes (`47d57be` + clip-fix follow-up).** Panning far enough that an axis left the view lost all positional reference. JSXGraph's native `position: 'sticky'` axes (`anchor: 'left right'`) pin to the nearest visible edge and keep their number labels; normal at the default view. **Follow-up (author report):** at 25px inset the pinned y-axis drew its left-extending labels off the frame — negatives/multi-digit clipped to the ones place. `ticksAutoPos` is NOT the fix (per JSXGraph source it pushes labels toward the *near* edge — wrong way for edge-pinning); widening `anchorDist` to 55px keeps them on-board. Re-verified: no left-clip on negatives panned to x∈[41,61]; centered view unchanged.

**Ops / delivery learnings this session (kit re-hashes on every change).** Set up **`.env.r2`** (gitignored) auto-loaded by `pnpm build:graph-kit` via `node --env-file-if-exists`, so uploads need no pasted creds (`6e48e65`). Two more build-script guards after live mistakes: placeholder/smart-quote/`cfut_`-token-as-secret all now fail with a plain message (`089c1d9`/`ed39bc9`/`6cff3fb` from the prior session, extended here). Hard rule learned: **upload the kit to R2 BEFORE redeploying `publish-activity`** — the reverse pointed the live function at a not-yet-uploaded hash and 404'd the summon button. Kit entry ended the session at `graph-kit-ZMCQEGJA.js` (270.8 KiB gz).

Suite through the whole run: schema 120 / graph-kit 71 / renderer 298 / app 257; typecheck/lint/build green. **Next:** Stage 5 (graded graph interactions) or Stage 4 polish (a `,` keypad key for touch-typed points; spreadsheet paste into the data table).

## 2026-07-05 (later) — Board navigation UX + full Stages 3–4 browser pass

Author live-tested and reported graph navigation "awful" → root cause: **JSXGraph's default mouse pan requires shift+drag** (we set `needTwoFingers: false` for touch but never `needShift: false` for mouse). The pass (`3e12143`): plain click-drag pan; on-graph **+ / − / reset** buttons (real labelled `<button>`s over the SVG, pointer-down stopped so a press can't start a pan); zoom centered on the current view (origin-relative zoom "runs away" after panning — keyboard +/- fixed too); **drag-resizable panel** (native CSS `resize: both`, 24×20rem–95vw/92vh; the flex chain + JSXGraph's ResizeObserver refit the board both axes); aria-live viewport readout now fires (debounced) on the board's `boundingbox` event, so mouse pan and wheel zoom are announced. Wheel zoom itself was already functional (JSXGraph listens for the legacy `mousewheel` event real input fires). `vite.config` gained a `PORT` env override so an agent preview server can run beside a manual one.

Same session, the **Stages 3–4 interactive pass completed** (agent-driven at `/dev/calculator`): regression on (1,4) (2,7) (3,12) (4,21) → `y = 2.3094 · 1.73563ˣ`, `r² = 0.9999` (the TI-84 values), Fit-view framing; `a=2` + `y=ax^2` re-plots live on slider drag; auto-append + palette colors confirmed. Also this session: graph-kit uploaded to R2 + `publish-activity` redeployed (entry `EQ5MV42W` verified HTTP 200) — then the UX pass minted entry `KXEOKBPT`, so a re-upload + redeploy is queued (STATE action #3). Three build-script guards landed en route (`089c1d9` placeholder creds, `ed39bc9` smart quotes, `6cff3fb` Token-value-vs-secret) after each mistake happened live.

## 2026-07-05 — Calculator Stage 4 (multi-expression list) — code-complete

The Desmos-defining surface (`f643087`): graphing mode swaps the single field for an **expression list** — N MathLive rows with color dots, each classified as a curve (`y =` stripped; bare expressions in x; constants = horizontal lines), a point (`(a, b)`, slider-aware coordinates), a **slider** (`a = 3`, single letter except x/y/e, constant RHS), or a student-safe error. Two-column layout (author green-lit): list + keypad left, board right; the Stage 3 data view swaps the left column via CSS.

- **Schema** — `maxExpressions` (optional int 1–50; ABSENT = unlimited, so stored docs never carry it unless a teacher caps). Editor gained an "Expression limit" input (blank = unlimited).
- **evaluate.ts** — `compileFunction` gained a `vars` scope param (reused scratch object — the slider-drag hot path allocates nothing per sample); new `classifyExpression()` with paren-group/top-level-comma parsing so `(x+1)*(x-2)` stays a function and `(1,2,3)` isn't a point. 15 new tests.
- **board.ts** — `PlotItem` + `setPlots()` (curves + points as live closures) + `refresh()`: slider drags mutate ONE stable scope object the closures capture, then re-sample in place — no reclassification, no object churn.
- **expression-list.ts (new, tool shell — layer 3a, calculator-only)** — lazy per-row reclassification (only changed text recompiles), Desmos-style trailing-row growth capped by `maxExpressions` (works from keypad input too — MathLive's programmatic insert doesn't reliably fire `input`, so the calculator calls `rebuild()` after keypad actions), per-row error notes, slider rows with auto-widening bounds and drag values that survive other rows' edits.
- **calculator.ts** — keypad types into the focused row; `=` inserts itself in graphing (for `y =` and `a = 3`) instead of evaluating; scientific mode untouched. Destroy blurs every row field (the MathLive teardown rule).

Suite: schema 120 / graph-kit 67 / renderer 298 / app 257; typecheck/lint/build green; renderer bundle + kit manifest regenerated (entry `graph-kit-EQ5MV42W.js`, 268.5 KiB gz — Stage 4 cost ~2 KiB). ⚠️ Interactive browser pass still pending (same dev-server port conflict as Stage 3) — exercise `/dev/calculator`: `a=2` then `y=ax^2` should plot and re-plot live on slider drag; keypad `x` key; cap behavior with maxExpressions=3. Known v1 gap: the keypad has no `,` key, so touch-only students can't type a point (physical keyboard works) — candidate fix in a Stage 4 polish pass. **Next: Stage 5** — graded interactions (the question shell, `interactive-graph-block.md`).

## 2026-07-04 — Calculator Stage 3 (data table + regression) — code-complete

The Texas Algebra I requirement: data table → model dropdown → equation + r² → fit curve over the scatter, as a **Data view inside graphing mode** (green-lit over a third top-level mode — it pre-figures Stage 4's expression-list layout and the `scientific` ceiling naturally excludes it). Manual (x, y) entry only in v1 (green-lit; spreadsheet paste is a possible fast-follow). Two commits: `2bc3f68` (schema flag + engine), `9df8738` (widget + editor).

- **Schema (`2bc3f68`)** — `CalculatorRestrictions.allowedRegressionModels` (`RegressionModel` enum array; default all three, **empty = regression off**). Additive/defaulted → stored docs parse unchanged, no `schemaVersion` bump; the renderer's `data-calculator-config` carries it for free (it serializes `restrictions` whole).
- **regression.ts (new, pure)** — closed-form least squares behind `fitModel()`: linear `y=ax+b`, quadratic via a pivoted 3×3 normal-equations solve, exponential `y=a·bˣ` fitted log-linearly. **TI-84/Desmos conventions pinned in tests**: exponential r² reported on the log-transformed fit (a different r² than the handheld next to the student breaks "feel at home" instantly); quadratic labeled R². Student-safe errors — `y ≤ 0` on exponential gets a clear message, not NaN. Standalone by design: the future graded regression block scores with the SAME engine.
- **data-table.ts (new, standalone kit piece)** — real `<table>` of number inputs, Desmos-style auto-append on last-row typing, per-row remove that never deletes the final row (clears it instead). Behavior pinned in 7 jsdom tests (app package).
- **fit-format.ts (new, pure)** — textbook equation strings (continuation signs, U+2212, unicode superscripts) + the r²/R² line; 6-sig-digit coefficients. Unit-tested.
- **board.ts** — `setScatter()`, `plotFit()` (dashed green — distinct from the student's own blue curve), `fitView()` (frame the data, 10% margin, degenerate-span guard).
- **calculator.ts** — a `Data` header toggle (graphing mode only, hidden when `allowedRegressionModels` is empty) flips `data-view` on the panel: the field + keypad hide and a two-column layout appears (table + model select + "Fit view" + aria-live results | board). **CSS-only view switch, no DOM reparenting** — JSXGraph keeps its container. Config read permissively (garbled array → all models; unknowns filtered, canonical order).
- **Editor (`9df8738`)** — `CalculatorSection` gains per-model checkboxes under a "Regression (data panel)" group (canonical order regardless of click order; visible only in graphing mode); the live preview re-mounts on model changes. `/dev/calculator` harness gained model toggles + a sample-data hint. Serialize is untouched opaque-carry (one test literal widened).

Verified: full suites green (schema 117 / graph-kit 52 / renderer 298 / app 257), typecheck + lint + production build clean, Vite module-transform smoke against the running dev server. ⚠️ **Interactive browser pass pending** — a second session's dev server held the preview port this session; exercise `/dev/calculator` (enter (1,4) (2,7) (3,12) (4,21) → Exponential should read y = 2.3094 · 1.7356ˣ, r² = 0.9999) before calling the stage fully verified. **Next: Stage 4** — multi-expression list (N rows, sliders, points, color coding).

## 2026-06-23 — Calculator Stage 2 (graphing, JSXGraph lazy-split) — shipped

Single-function graphing for the calculator (`5e513ac`). A spike found **JSXGraph is ~240 KB gz / 192 KB br** (939 KiB min — 2× the design doc's stale "~120 KB"), so rather than bundle it into the kit, it's **code-split into its own chunk loaded only in graphing mode** (the locked "lazy-split" decision): scientific-only calculators stay ~264 KB gz; a graphing calculator pulls the JSXGraph chunk (+240 KB gz) on demand and caches it.

- **evaluate.ts** — `compileFunction(expr)` returns `(x) => number` (math.compile in `x`; deg/rad + restriction gates; NaN at out-of-domain so the curve breaks rather than emitting garbage). 6 tests.
- **board.ts (new)** — the board layer: `createBoard()` → `{ plot, destroy }` over JSXGraph (axes/grid, drag-pan, wheel-zoom) + baseline a11y (`role`/focusable, arrow-key pan + `+`/`-` zoom, an aria-live viewport readout). It STATICALLY imports JSXGraph; everything else dynamic-imports `board.ts`, which is what makes esbuild split JSXGraph out. Deliberately NOT re-exported from index.ts (that would static-import it and defeat the split).
- **calculator.ts** — graphing mode swaps the numeric result for the board, lazy-imports `board.ts`, plots the live expression (a constant → a horizontal line); an `x` variable key replaces `!` in graphing mode; RAD is the default angle for graphing (degrees flattens `sin(x)` over a default window).
- **build-graph-kit.mjs** — esbuild `splitting:true` → entry `graph-kit-<hash>.js` + `graph-kit-chunk-<hash>.js` (JSXGraph) + a tiny shared chunk; the manifest records the ENTRY (matched on `index.ts`, since esbuild marks `entryPoint` on dynamic-import targets too — an early bug had it pick the JSXGraph chunk); uploads every `.js` to `shared/`. The entry references its chunks by relative URL (resolves same-origin on R2).
- **editor** — a Mode selector (scientific/graphing) in `CalculatorSection`; the live preview reflects it. `/dev/calculator` harness gained a mode toggle.

Browser-verified via `/dev/calculator`: plots x² (parabola) and sin(x), re-plots on change, arrow-key pan + `+` zoom announce the viewport via aria-live, the `x` key builds x². Suite: schema 113 / graph-kit 32 / renderer 298 / app 250; typecheck clean. **Next:** Stage 3 — data table + regression (linear/quad/exp), the Texas Algebra I requirement; the least-squares engine + table stay standalone so the future graded-regression block reuses them.
