## 2026-06-21 — Calculator tool (Phase 2.7, Stage 1: scientific) — code-complete

The first face of the shared graphing kit (built bottom-up; the graded interactive-graph block drops onto the same kit later). Design + locked decisions in [docs/design/calculator-tool.md](../docs/design/calculator-tool.md). A teacher can enable + restrict an on-screen scientific calculator per activity; students summon it while working; it's a scaffold — never scored, no submission, no answer key.

**Bundle-weight spike (the locked-decision keystone).** Measured through esbuild against real MathLive 0.109.2: MathLive input is a fixed ~219 KiB-gzip floor; the evaluator swings ~280 KiB (Compute Engine +283, math.js-number +42, hand-rolled +1). **Locked:** evaluator = **math.js number-only** behind a single `evaluate()` seam (Compute Engine = drop-in escape hatch); delivery = **one shared content-hashed kit on R2**, lazy-`import()`ed on first summon; fonts via jsDelivr (matching KaTeX; the "fonts on R2" idea was dropped — jsDelivr is the one source that works identically in app + published without context detection).

- **Schema slice (`77ff8e3`).** `CalculatorTool`/`CalculatorRestrictions` in `packages/schema/src/document.ts`, `calculator?` on `ActivityDocument`, `createCalculatorTool()` factory, 12 tests. `mode` enum `scientific|graphing` (defaults `scientific` — the only Stage-1 capability); permissive restriction defaults; no `schemaVersion` bump.
- **Renderer slice (`d7cf6cf`).** `renderCalculatorTool()` emits the cheap half — `<div class="calculator-tool" data-block-category="scaffold">` (summon button + empty mount + `data-calculator-{mode,config,kit-src}`), gated on `doc.calculator.enabled` AND `RenderContext.calculatorKitUrl` (absent → omitted, graceful). A ~0.8 KiB `runtime/calculator-summon.ts` sidecar (inlined only when a calculator is emitted) lazy-imports the heavy widget on first click. RUNTIME.md contract + 5-build pipeline updated; print CSS hides it; 8 renderer tests.
- **Widget slice (`9f82383`).** New **`@activity/graph-kit`** package (mathlive + mathjs). `evaluate.ts` — the engine-agnostic seam: normalizes MathLive AsciiMath → math.js, deg/rad-aware + restriction-gated + correctly-named ln/log10 functions; **26 unit tests against real `convertLatexToAsciiMath` output** pin the "is the glue leaky?" risk (it isn't). `calculator.ts` `mountCalculator()` — MathLive field + scientific keypad + live result + deg/rad toggle + `allowTrig`/`allowLogExp` key gating, non-modal floating panel that injects its own styles; returns `{toggle,isOpen,open,close,destroy}`. Browser-verified via a dev-only `/dev/calculator` harness. **Fixed a destroy-while-focused MathLive crash** (removing a focused field fires `onBlur` against a dead model → blur before remove; don't steal focus on remount).
- **Build-hosting slice (`f581685`).** `scripts/build-graph-kit.mjs` (`pnpm build:graph-kit`) bundles the kit → content-hashed `graph-kit-<hash>.js` (967 KiB / 264 KiB gz, matching the spike), writes the committed manifest `_shared/graph-kit-manifest.ts`, and PUTs to R2 `shared/` only when R2 creds are in env (idempotent; no-creds runs build+manifest only). `publish-activity` reads the manifest → `calculatorKitUrl`. Brotli via Cloudflare edge.
- **Editor slice (`cfb8f04`).** `tiptapToActivity` gains a `calculator?` opaque-carry arg (parallel to `referencePanel`). A `CalculatorSection` disclosure (🧮) in the editor settings sidebar: enable toggle + `allowTrig`/`allowLogExp` checkboxes + a **live preview** mounting the same `mountCalculator()` students get (re-mounts on flag change). `calculator` state loads from `doc.calculator`, folds into the autosave fingerprint, and saves through. 5 serialize round-trip tests.

Suite at completion: schema 113 / graph-kit 26 / renderer 298 / app 250; typecheck clean. **To go live:** run `pnpm build:graph-kit` with R2 creds (upload), commit the manifest, redeploy `publish-activity` (STATE pending action). **Graded regression** captured as a future variant of the *graded* face ([interactive-graph-block.md](../docs/design/interactive-graph-block.md)); not scheduled. **Next:** Stage 2 — single-function graphing (JSXGraph board layer).

## Snapshot as of 2026-06-11 — "Current focus" narratives

(Verbatim from STATE.md when this archive was created: print feature Drops A–D, structural columns, the Phase 2 rich-text slice, Stage 16 + hardening pass, Stage 15 + answerFeedback.)

**Print feature — teacher-configurable printables (Drops A–D) — COMPLETE (code + tests; GUI-tested by author this session).** The whole feature follows the "one renderer, not two" principle: print is the same `ActivityDocument`→HTML path, with print config producing classes / CSS vars / data-attrs consumed under `@media print`. Schema additions are optional-with-defaults, so **no `schemaVersion` bump, no migration**. Built client-side where possible — the print route imports `@activity/renderer` directly via Vite, so it needs **no Edge Function and no redeploy**.

- **Drop A — schema + renderer foundation (`1ba8a34`).** `PrintConfig` + `PrintHeader` on `ActivityMeta` (`print: PrintConfig.default({})`), per-block `FillInBlankBlock.workSpace`. Renderer emits a per-document `<style>@page{size;margin}</style>` (paperSize+margin can't be CSS vars — `@page` can't read them), `--print-*` container vars (columns/workSpace/fontSize/problemSpacing) read by `@media print`, a print-only header (Name/Date/… fill-in lines), and `renderActivityForPrint` — the print document: same body, none of the interactive chrome.
- **Drop B — editor authoring UI + print confidence (`fca4a3e`).** A "🖨 Print & worksheet layout" disclosure in `ActivityEditor` (paper/columns/margin/font/spacing/workSpace + header-field toggles + comma-separated custom fields); a per-problem "Print work space" control in `FillInBlankView`. `meta.print` round-trips **for free** (`tiptapToActivity` embeds `meta` whole); only per-block `workSpace` needed a Tiptap attr + serialize. Plus a **hand-markable confidence row** that prints (paper twin of the interactive `.js-confidence-rating` fieldset, which is print-hidden) — fixes "space reserved but nothing rendered."
- **Drop C — the print route (`12a1071`).** `/activity/:id/print`: client-side, three-way load (draft → published version → error, mirroring the editor), an `<iframe srcDoc>` that IS the print source (Print button calls its `contentWindow.print()`), session-only layout overrides + Reset (shadow the saved config without touching it), and a "Show answers" answer-key variant (`renderActivityForPrint(doc, { showAnswers })` prefills blanks with their canonical answer).
- **Drop D — the journal foldable (THIS commit).** A "Journal foldable" **layout option** in the print route, built **client-side, DOM-measured (Route 2)** — never on the published static page. New engine under `packages/app/src/lib/foldable/`: `geometry` (PrintConfig → landscape sheet → one panel's inch/px budget) → `measure` (offscreen iframe reports each block's printed height) → `paginate` (greedy vertical packing into fixed panels, never splits a problem) → `compose` (duplex booklet imposition) → `render` (assemble the printable HTML). The load-bearing invariant: **measure === print**, guaranteed by one shared stylesheet (`styles.ts`) used in both the measuring iframe and the printed document, which promotes the renderer's `@media print` rules to unconditional. **Confirmed geometry:** single vertical fold, 4 half-sheet panels on a landscape sheet (2 per duplex face), reading order `[content #1, #2, #3, blank glue tab]` — **glue tab is always the fixed back cover**; **overflow spills into multiple self-contained foldables** (3 content panels each). Required re-exporting `blockStyles` + `katexCss` from `@activity/renderer` (additive; the app imports renderer source, so no rebuild). Pure core (`paginate`/`compose`) is unit-tested; the DOM glue is GUI-tested by the author (playground, satisfactory).
- **Tests + builds green:** app **75** (10 new foldable tests) / renderer **225**; `tsc -b`, app vite build all clean. ⚠ **Pre-existing lint errors remain in `FillInBlank.ts:6`** (unused `Options`/`Storage` generics in a module augmentation — red since Drop B `fca4a3e`, NOT from this work) — flagged for a separate one-line fix (eslint-disable / repo ignore-pattern).
- **No deploy needed.** The print route + foldable are entirely client-side. The renderer-bundle / `publish-activity` redeploy path is untouched (the published page's baseline print CSS already shipped pre-Drop-A; `showAnswers` is print-route-only and never reaches `publish-activity`).

**Structural authored columns — COMPLETE** (schema+renderer `ee3c544`, editor+grid-lines `e16d3b3`): a side-by-side container block so columns are part of the *content* and render consistently online + in worksheet print + inside a foldable panel, with an optional ruled-grid mode (per-block tri-state + activity-wide print default). ⚠ Needs a `publish-activity` redeploy to reach published pages. See Nearest next steps for the full writeup and the next goal (columns polish + variable block sizing).

**Phase 2 (first slice) — rich text + inline math in popover/settings fields — is COMPLETE (code + tests; live UX unverified).** The blank `hint`, blank `mistakeFeedback[].feedback`, and FillInBlank `solution` fields now accept inline rich text (bold/italic/code/underline/sub/sup) and inline KaTeX — matching how problem prose already renders. Scope is **inline-only** (no block content), the balance the author approved.

- **Editor stores canonical `InlineNode[]`.** These three attrs hold the schema's inline form directly (Tiptap node attrs carry it opaquely; `Blank.ts`/`FillInBlank.ts` parse/render it via `data-hint`/`data-mistake-feedback`/`data-solution` JSON). So `serialize.ts` is a trivial pass-through — it no longer coerces strings. New reusable `InlineRichTextEditor.tsx` (nested Tiptap, StarterKit trimmed to inline marks + `Subscript`/`Superscript`/`MathInline`) owns Tiptap↔InlineNode conversion via newly-exported `activityInlineToTiptap`/`tiptapInlineToActivity` + `InlineNodes` type from serialize. `BlankEditPopover` and `FillInBlankView` render it (keyed by `blankId` so the uncontrolled editor remounts only on retarget; live-commit never resets the caret).
- **Server pre-renders, client clones (KaTeX stays server-side).** Same pattern as prose: the renderer pre-renders rich popover/solution content into hidden `<template>` nodes; the runtime clones them client-side, so the ~280 KiB KaTeX never ships to students. Runtime bundle regenerated — **14.8 KiB** (budget 20). `runtime-bundle.ts` + `supabase/functions/_shared/renderer.bundle.js` rebuilt.
- **Tests + builds green:** schema 30 / renderer 212 / app 63; `tsc -b`, app vite build, `pnpm -r typecheck` all clean. Pre-existing unrelated lint errors remain in `FillInBlank.ts:6` (unused `Options`/`Storage` generics — confirmed on main via `git stash`).
- **⚠ Two follow-ups (author):** (1) **`supabase functions deploy publish-activity`** is required for the new rich content to go live on published pages — until then the bundle change is committed but not deployed. (2) **Browser UX verification of the nested mini-editors is still pending** (caret behavior, math insertion, virtual-keyboard interplay with the popover) — covered by code/tests but not yet exercised live.

**Stage 16 — Submissions dashboard — is COMPLETE and live-verified.** A per-activity dashboard at `/activity/:id/submissions` with a Summary/All-attempts toggle, per-student grouping (latest headline + best secondary + attempt count), and an expandable drill-down that maps each blank response back to its problem prompt/answer key plus per-section checkpoint results. See Nearest next steps #6 for the full design + the two deviations from the original plan (latest-not-best headline; `activityType` filter dropped).

The author ran the live pass this session; three issues surfaced and were fixed:
- **(a) Published-URL retrieval was hard.** The live R2 URL was only shown in the post-publish `success` state of `PublishControl` — reopening a published activity gave no way to get the link. Added a persistent `View published page · Copy link` affordance in the editor header (`ActivityEditor.tsx`), gated on `current_version_id`, building `{VITE_PUBLISHED_URL_BASE}/{id}/index.html`. **New client env var `VITE_PUBLISHED_URL_BASE`** mirrors the publish function's `R2_PUBLIC_URL_BASE` secret (write-only on Supabase, so it had to be duplicated client-side); unset → the link hides rather than rendering a broken URL. Set in `.env.local` (gitignored); documented in `.env.local.example`.
- **(b) Blank-settings authoring was undiscoverable.** The `⚙ Settings` footer in `FillInBlankView.tsx` was gated on Tiptap's `selected` prop, which is only true for a NodeSelection of the whole block — clicking *into* a problem makes a TextSelection inside it, so `selected` stayed false and the worked-solution/confidence panel never revealed. Now shown on **every** fill-in-blank while the editor is editable (`showFooter = isEditable || isConfigured`).
- **(c) Student submissions 401'd** with `{"code":"UNAUTHORIZED_NO_AUTH_HEADER"}`. `ingest-submission` had been deployed with `verify_jwt: true`, so the platform gateway rejected anonymous student POSTs before reaching the function. **Redeployed via CLI with `--no-verify-jwt`** (`supabase functions deploy ingest-submission --no-verify-jwt --project-ref dtqutpdplefmufrrakxs`) → now `verify_jwt: false`. No code change — the function self-authenticates with the service role and validates in its body + the `ingest_submission` RPC. There is no `supabase/config.toml`, so this flag lives only on the platform; re-set it on any future CLI redeploy of this function.

Also seeded **3 `[TEST]` activities + 11 submissions** straight into the live DB to exercise the dashboard (generator validated every doc/blob through the real Zod schema before insert; re-runnable SQL at [scripts/seed-test-data.sql](scripts/seed-test-data.sql), idempotent teardown at top). Covers multi-attempt grouping, latest≠best (Carlos regresses), confidence, checkpoints, v1 migrate-on-read, removed-blank, unreadable-responses, null-score edge cases. Note: seeded rows are `published` but their HTML was never uploaded to R2, so their `View published page` link 404s until a real Publish.

### Post-Stage-16 hardening pass (code review)

A `/code-review` of the Stage 16 diff surfaced ~10 findings; all addressed. Most were internal-correctness fixes in `Submissions.tsx` / `submissions.ts` (no behavior or context change): synchronous `loadState` reset on activity switch so back/forward doesn't flash the prior activity's rows; NaN-safe checkpoint sort (`Infinity - Infinity`) and deterministic blank/problem tiebreaks; coerce `score` from PostgREST's `numeric`-as-**string** to a real number at the query boundary; float-safe `formatScore` (snap to 4-dp before scaling so `0.575*100` doesn't drop a half-percent); plus three hardened test fixtures. App **63 tests + tsc green.**

The one finding that **changes context** is the answer-key version-skew fix:
- **Submissions are now pinned to the version the student answered.** New migration **[0007_submission_version.sql](supabase/migrations/0007_submission_version.sql)** adds a nullable `submissions.activity_version_id` (→ `activity_versions(id) on delete set null`), backfills existing rows to each activity's `current_version_id`, and `create or replace`s `ingest_submission` to record `current_version_id` on every new row (+ in the audit-log metadata). **Signature unchanged → `ingest-submission` Edge Function needs NO redeploy.**
- **The dashboard indexes each submission against its own version.** `Submissions.tsx` now builds one `ActivityIndex` per referenced version (loaded via a single `.in('id', …)` on `activity_versions`) and resolves per-row via `resolveIndex(row.activity_version_id)`, falling back to the current version for null/legacy/unparseable rows. So the drill-down shows the answer key the student actually saw — correct even after a republish (previously it always used the current version). `SubmissionRow` gained `activity_version_id: string | null`.
- ⚠ **Migration 0007 is written but NOT yet applied.** The author applies it (`supabase db push` / dashboard) — same as git pushes. Until applied, every row's `activity_version_id` is null and the dashboard falls back to the current version (identical to pre-fix behavior, no regression). After apply, only *new* submissions get true per-version accuracy; pre-migration rows can't be retroactively attributed (that data was never recorded) and stay backfilled to the current version.

**Next:** Apply migration 0007 (author). Then Phase 1 polish / print feature.

**Stage 15 — Editor UI for remaining feature fields — is COMPLETE.** Activity-level metadata pickers and FillInBlank block-level field authoring exist in the editor; the locked-mode checkpoint validation warns (non-blocking).

What landed this stage (editor + serialize only — NO renderer/runtime/bundle change; the renderer already emitted `data-solution`/`data-has-confidence-rating`/`data-skills` and the runtime already consumed them, so this stage only made the editor *produce* those fields):

- **Activity-settings panel (`ActivityEditor.tsx`).** A collapsible "⚙ Activity settings" disclosure under the title input. Three `<select>` pickers — `submissionMode` (single/locked/free), `revisionMode` (free/locked), `activityType` (worksheet/exit_ticket/warm_up/review) — wired straight to `setMeta`. Meta already round-tripped through `draft_content` and `changeKey` already fingerprinted it, so autosave picks up changes for free. `revisionMode` is **disabled with explanatory text when `submissionMode === 'single'`** (schema ignores it there). `gradingMode` deliberately **omitted** (inert in Phase 1 — a picker would imply behavior that doesn't exist).
- **Locked-mode checkpoint warning.** Amber banner under the settings panel, shown when `submissionMode === 'locked'` AND `hasNonCheckpointSection(tiptap)` is true (any section — including the implicit leading run before the first `sectionBreak` — that isn't a checkpoint). **Warns, does not block save** (addresses open-question #258). The walk mirrors `splitTiptapBlocksIntoSections` in serialize.ts.
- **FillInBlank block-level fields (`FillInBlank.ts` + `FillInBlankView.tsx`).** Added `solution` / `hasConfidenceRating` / `skills` attrs (parse/render via `data-solution`/`data-has-confidence-rating`/`data-skills`). A footer disclosure in the NodeView (contentEditable={false}, hidden when the block is plain + unselected + unconfigured) exposes a `solution` textarea and an "Ask for a confidence rating" checkbox. Document concerns write via `updateAttributes`; only the open/closed state is React `useState` (5-commitments rule).
- **Serialize round-trip (`serialize.ts`).** New `tiptapFillInBlankToActivity` carries `solution` (only when non-empty), `hasConfidenceRating`, and `skills` into the ActivityDocument — replacing the old hardcoded `hasConfidenceRating: false`/`skills: []`/dropped-solution. `activityFillInBlankToTiptap` now emits all three attrs (explicit defaults `''`/`false`/`[]`) so the editor round-trips them.
- **`skills` UI deferred to Phase 2** (per the schema's own scheduling — every skills comment reads "Editor UI for this field is Phase 2"). The attr still round-trips through serialize and the node so any imported/future-authored tags survive; there is just no editing control. Confirmed with the author.
- **Tests + verification.** Added 3 serialize round-trip tests for the block-level fields (round-trip, into-ActivityDocument, defaults-absent). App **50 tests green**; `pnpm --filter @activity/app build` clean (tsc -b + vite). No bundle regen (no renderer/runtime change). **Live-UI pass DONE** — author exercised the editor settings + `answerFeedback` picker on a real authed activity; features work.

### Follow-on: `answerFeedback` mode (configurable answer validation)

The green-correct / red-wrong blank validation used to be **always on** (every blank self-checked on blur). The author flagged this should be opt-in. Resolved as a **separate, orthogonal `ActivityMeta` field** (`answerFeedback: 'immediate' | 'on_check'`, schema default `'on_check'`) — NOT a fourth `submissionMode`, mirroring how `submissionMode`/`revisionMode` are kept as independent enums.

- `'immediate'` — blur scores the blank; student sees correct/incorrect right away (the old always-on behavior).
- `'on_check'` — correctness stays hidden until a section check or final submit (the **new default** for fresh activities).

What changed (this **DID** touch renderer/runtime/bundle, unlike Stage 15 proper):
- **Schema** (`document.ts`, `factories.ts`): added `answerFeedback` enum (default `on_check`); factory constructs it explicitly.
- **Renderer** (`document.ts`): config blob now carries `answerFeedback`.
- **Runtime** (`config.ts`, `blanks.ts`, `index.ts`): `RuntimeConfig.answerFeedback`; `parseConfig` treats it as **not-required** and **coerces missing/invalid → `'immediate'`** (pre-field published pages keep their original self-check-on-blur behavior — schema default and runtime fallback intentionally differ). `wireBlanks(answerFeedback, …)` only attaches the blur-scoring handler in `'immediate'` mode; the input/edit-to-clear handler runs in **both** modes (so editing after a section check still clears a stale border).
- **Editor** (`ActivityEditor.tsx`): a 4th picker in the settings disclosure (`Reveal on check` / `Immediate self-check`); settings grid widened to `sm:grid-cols-2`.
- **Tests:** schema default + explicit + invalid-reject; `parseConfig` preserve/missing→immediate/invalid→immediate; `wireBlanks` blur-gating in both modes + edit-to-clear in on_check. Schema 30 / renderer 207 / app 50 green; all builds clean. RUNTIME.md §7 `wireBlanks` signature updated.
- **Bundle regenerated** (`pnpm run bundle:renderer`) — runtime source changed, so `runtime-bundle.ts` + `supabase/functions/_shared/renderer.bundle.js` are rebuilt. ✅ **`publish-activity` redeployed** — new behavior is live on published pages.

## Columns-polish / image-authoring work log + Stage writeups

(Verbatim from STATE.md "Nearest next steps" at archive time. The numbered stage items 1–6 are all complete; item-level detail kept for reference.)

**Structural authored columns — COMPLETE (code + tests; browser-verified in the playground).** A *content* primitive replaces the per-mode CSS `Columns` print settings: the teacher places blocks side by side, so columns render identically everywhere — on screen, in worksheet print, and inside a foldable panel. Two commits:
   - **Schema + renderer foundation (`ee3c544`).** `ColumnsBlock` (`columns: Column[]`, `.min(2).max(6)`) + `Column` (`blocks: ColumnCellBlock[]`, enumerated cell types that **forbid columns-in-columns**, optional per-column `width` weight). Renderer emits a CSS grid with track sizing from a single `--columns-template` custom property (so a media query can override it); `@media screen and (max-width:640px)` collapses to one column while print/foldable stay multi-column. Nested problems number **column-major** (cells walked in array order, sharing the one `nextProblemNumber` closure).
   - **Editor + grid-lines option (`e16d3b3`, this work).** Native Tiptap `Columns`/`Column` nodes (renderHTML/parseHTML + CSS, **not** a React NodeView — sidesteps the Stage-13.5 NodeView reconciliation hazard), insert via slash menu ("2/3 columns") + a toolbar button, `serialize.ts` round-trip. Plus the **grid-lines** feature (author asked for "cells + dividers" + "both"): a per-block tri-state override (`gridLines: inherit|on|off`) **and** an activity-wide print default (`meta.print.gridLines`). The renderer resolves the tri-state and emits `data-grid-lines`, boxing the block + ruling between cells and between stacked blocks in a cell; ruled cells `align-items:stretch` so the between-cell divider spans the full box (fixed a truncated-divider bug where an empty cell cut the rule short). Editor surfaces a contextual "Grid: auto/on/off" toolbar toggle and a "Grid lines on columns blocks" print-settings checkbox; `Columns.configure({ gridLinesDefault })` threads the activity default into the preview (fixed at editor mount).
   - **Schema additive — no `schemaVersion` bump, no migration.** Tests: schema 45 / renderer 235 / app 83, all green; app build clean.
   - ⚠ **`publish-activity` redeploy required to go live.** The renderer bundle (`supabase/functions/_shared/renderer.bundle.js`) was regenerated with the new columns + grid-lines CSS and committed, but `publish-activity` imports that bundle — **author runs `supabase functions deploy publish-activity`** so newly-published pages get the grid CSS. No DB migration. Until redeployed, the feature works in the editor/preview but published HTML carries the old bundle's CSS.

**▶ Active next goal — columns polish + variable block sizing.** The container exists; the remaining work is authoring affordances and the broader sizing vision it was built for:
   - **Add/remove-column controls — DONE (code + live-verified; committed `7b77da6`).** `addColumn`/`removeColumn` commands on the `Columns` extension (`Columns.ts`) wired to two contextual toolbar buttons ("+ Column" / "− Column") that mirror the existing Grid toggle. `addColumn` inserts beside the cell holding the cursor (or appends when the whole block is node-selected); `removeColumn` removes the cell holding the cursor (or the last when node-selected). Both use a shared `findColumnsTarget(state)` selection locator (handles text-cursor-in-cell via `$from.before/after(depth)` and `NodeSelection`-on-block). Schema's 2–6 bounds enforced in the commands (return false at min/max), which also drives the toolbar `disabled` state via `editor.can()`. No NodeView (respects the standing constraint). App build + 83 tests green; live-tested in the playground (caps at 6, floors at 2, cursor stays in a cell across edits, no console errors). No new unit tests — headless Tiptap command tests need a jsdom harness that doesn't exist (same precedent as Stage 13.5 editor work; flagged as housekeeping).
   - **Per-column width presets — DONE (code + tests + live-verified).** A `cycleColumnWidths` command on the `Columns` extension (`Columns.ts`) wired to a contextual "Width" toolbar button mirroring the Grid toggle. Cycles the active block through count-specific presets: 2-col = even / wide-left / wide-right; 3-col adds wide-center; **4–6-col are even-only** so the command returns false and the button disables (still showing "Width: even"). Presets map to per-column `width` fr-weights (even ⇒ all null; wide ⇒ a single `2` on the emphasised column, others null ⇒ 1fr in the renderer), so the editor stores the minimum and the renderer — which already consumes `width` via `--columns-template` since `ee3c544` — honours them **with no bundle regen / redeploy** (editor-authoring only). The editor itself still lays cells equally (`grid-auto-columns:1fr`) by design; width shows in the published/print output, not the editing canvas. Preset logic (`widthPresetOrder`/`presetToWidths`/`detectWidthPreset`) is extracted as pure exported helpers and unit-tested (8 tests in `columns-width.test.ts`); the command's DOM glue is live-verified in the playground (full cycles at counts 2/3, disabled at 4, no console errors). App build + **91 tests** green.
   - **Width visual picker + editor preview + narrow presets — DONE (code + tests + live-verified).** Three upgrades to the width-preset feature above. (1) **The editor canvas now previews the real widths**: `.editor-columns` switched from an equal-split CSS grid (`grid-auto-columns:1fr`) to a **flexbox** row, and each `Column.renderHTML` emits `flex-grow:<weight>` from its stored `width` (flex-grow with basis 0 ≈ the renderer's fr tracks), so a wide/narrow pick is visible while authoring — editor and published output now match. Grid-lines CSS adapted to flex (all selectors were child-based; only the container display changed). (2) **New `narrow-*` presets at count 3** (`narrow-left/center/right`, weight `0.5`): "make one column narrower" as the complement to wide; not offered at count 2 (a narrow-X there is the same ratio as wide-other). Schema/renderer unchanged — `width: z.number().positive()` already accepts `0.5` and the renderer already emits `0.5fr`, so **no bundle regen / redeploy**. (3) **Visual picker** (`ColumnWidthPicker.tsx`) replaces the cycling "Width" toolbar button: a dropdown of layout **thumbnails** (bars whose flex-grow mirrors each preset, emphasised column tinted indigo) the author clicks directly, driven by a new `setColumnWidthPreset(preset)` command (direct-set counterpart to `cycleColumnWidths`, which is retained). Trigger shows the current short label + caret, disables off a columns block and for even-only 4–6-col. Preset helpers extended (`WidthPreset` union + `widthPresetOrder`/`presetToWidths`/`detectWidthPreset` now handle narrow; wide wins over narrow on arbitrary imported weights). Pure helpers unit-tested (**11 tests** in `columns-width.test.ts`, +3); app build + **99 tests** green. Live-verified in playground: seeded 3-col block previews `flex-grow` 1/2/1; cursor-in-cell enables the picker reading "wide C"; dropdown lists all 7 options with the current one checked; picking "Narrow right" sets the 3rd cell `flex-grow:0.5`, closes the menu, updates the trigger, and stores `"width": 0.5` in the JSON.
   - **Image authoring — Drop 1 DONE (code + tests + live-verified; uncommitted at time of writing → committed this session).** A structural `image` block (Tiptap node `Image.ts`, group `block`, `atom`, `draggable`) authored via a clean **anchored-popover** experience that reuses the Stage-13.5 single-root-host pattern (no per-node popover mounting). In-editor look is **Option B: a compact placeholder card** (`ImageView.tsx`, React NodeView) showing an icon + title (alt ‖ filename ‖ "Add image") + path/URL + "Edit"; empty-src cards render dashed with "Add image / Click to set a URL or upload". `ImagePopoverHost.tsx` watches the selection for a node-selected `image` and mounts `ImageEditPopover.tsx` (floating-ui + focus-trap, save-on-blur + flush-on-close, `preserveSelection` to stay open through edits) with **two tabs: Paste URL (live) + Upload (stub — "File upload is coming next")**. Fields: src/alt/caption. Inserted via toolbar ("Image", always enabled) + slash menu (keywords image/picture/photo/figure/img/media); allowed inside columns (Column content spec extended). `serialize.ts` round-trips image ↔ `ImageBlock` (drops empty-src nodes with a warn; caption only when non-empty). Renderer already had `renderImage` (`figure.block-image` + lazy/async img + optional figcaption) — **no renderer/runtime change, no bundle regen, no redeploy** (editor-authoring only; the published img tag was already supported). 5 new serialize tests (round-trip src+alt+caption, empty-caption omission, empty-src drop, schema-valid intermediate, image-nested-in-column); app **96 tests** + build green. Live-verified in playground: insert → empty selected card + open popover; URL/alt/caption commit updates card + JSON; Escape closes + releases; click-to-reopen sets NodeSelection; Upload tab shows the stub.
   - **Image authoring — Drop 2 DONE (code; ⚠ needs deploy + R2 CORS to go live).** New `upload-image` Supabase edge function (`supabase/functions/upload-image/index.ts`) mirroring `publish-activity`'s `aws4fetch` AwsClient R2 PUT: accepts `multipart/form-data { activity_id, file }`, verifies edit rights via the `can_edit_activity` RPC (user-scoped client → correct `auth.uid()`), validates MIME (PNG/JPEG/GIF/WebP/AVIF — **SVG excluded** as an XSS surface) + 10 MB cap, PUTs to `uploads/{activityId}/{uuid}.{ext}` (immutable cache), returns `{ url }` off `R2_PUBLIC_URL_BASE`. Reuses the same R2 secrets as `publish-activity` (no new env). Client: `packages/app/src/lib/uploadImage.ts` (session-token-attached `functions.invoke` with a FormData body, mirrors PublishControl's error-surfacing) + the popover's Upload tab now renders a real file picker with uploading/error states (`ImageEditPopover` gains `activityId`, threaded `ActivityEditor → Editor → ImagePopoverHost`; the **playground has no activityId so the tab shows a "needs a saved activity" note** — verified). On success it commits the returned URL as `src` with `preserveSelection` so the author can add alt/caption without the popover closing. App **96 tests** + build clean; upload path not playground-testable (needs the deployed function + an authed activity). **Author to deploy:** `supabase functions deploy upload-image`, and **add the SPA origin to the R2 bucket's CORS allowed-origins** (browser PUT is to the function, but the editor later loads the image from the r2.dev/custom-domain URL — confirm cross-origin `<img>` works; if a custom domain is used, no CORS needed for `<img>`).
   - **Image authoring — live preview DONE (code + build; playground DOM/CSS-verified).** Replaced the in-editor compact card with a **live preview of the actual image** once a `src` loads (`ImageView.tsx` rewrite): renders the `<img>` height-capped at 280px (`max-width:100% / object-fit:contain`) with a hover-only "Edit" chip and the caption beneath. The compact card is retained for two fallback states — **empty** (dashed "Add image") and **broken URL** (`onError` → amber-tinted `.image-card.is-broken` "Couldn't load — click to edit the URL"). `ImagePopoverHost` anchor selector relaxed from `.image-card[data-image-id]` to `[data-image-id]` so the popover anchors in every state. Editor-only — no serialize/renderer change. App build clean; 96 tests still green; verified in the playground (preview `<img>` loads with correct alt/caption, card replaced, popover anchor intact — pixel layout unmeasurable in the 0×0 headless viewport, CSS confirmed correct).
   - **Drag-between-cells — DONE (code + tests + DOM-verified; committed `6c946de`). ⚠ live drag gesture still needs a human pass.** Authors can drag a block from one column cell into another (and reorder within a cell), and move the whole columns block via a dedicated grip. Two parts. **(1) The inner-block hover handle** — the existing `@tiptap/extension-drag-handle-react` plus **one config object**, `packages/app/src/editor/dragHandleNested.ts` (`columnsNestedDragOptions`), passed as `<DragHandle nested={…}>` in `Editor.tsx` (2-line diff). The handle defaulted to top-level blocks only (hovering inside a columns block grabbed the *whole* block), so nested mode is enabled, scoped surgically with **`defaultRules: false`** + a single custom rule (`columnsOnlyDrill`); `edgeDetection: 'none'`. **The hard part was not reaching an inner block but reaching its HANDLE without the target flipping to the container.** The handle floats in the left gutter; to click it the cursor must leave the block's text and cross the cell's left padding (cell 1) or the inter-column gap (cell 2+), and those bands resolve to a *column-boundary* position (depth 2) whose only candidates are the bare cell (never dragged) and the columns container — so the handle flipped up to the container exactly as the author reached for it (the live-reported bug; an earlier `edgeDetection`-only attempt did NOT fix it). **Fix exploits a plugin property:** when the hover scan finds NO eligible candidate it returns early and KEEPS the node it had locked (no flip). So the rule yields *no candidate* in those padding/gap bands: a bare `column` is never a target; the `columns` container is excluded whenever the cursor is inside a cell (still eligible from a depth-1 position outside every cell as a harmless fallback); a deeper block is a target ONLY when the resolved position is inside a `column`. Net: hover an inner block → its handle appears; slide left to the handle → it stays on that inner block (gap/padding produce no rival). **(2) The whole-block grip** — instead of a geometric "rail" (v1's `.editor-columns { padding: 0.875rem 0 }`, which couldn't survive grid-lines mode without re-introducing top/bottom divider stubs, leaving the container ungrabbable there), a **dedicated always-present grip widget** (`columnsGrip` ProseMirror plugin in `extensions/Columns.ts`): a `Decoration.widget` at the block's first inner position renders a small drag dot-grid pinned to the block's top-left corner (`.editor-columns-grip`, `position:absolute; translate(-50%,-50%)`, dim 0.45 → 1 on hover). Its `dragstart` selects the `columns` node (`NodeSelection`) and seeds `view.dragging = { slice, move: true }` so PM's native drop moves the whole block; `stopPropagation` keeps the gesture off the inner handle. Works identically in default **and** grid-lines mode at zero print-fidelity cost, so the rail CSS was removed entirely. **No PM schema / isolating change** — native drop moves blocks across `isolating` cells and auto-backfills an emptied cell with a paragraph. Verification: pure rule core `columnsDragDeduction(nodeTypeName, depth, insideColumn)` unit-tested (7 tests in `dragHandleNested.test.ts`, app **106 tests** + build green); the library's scoring + `clampToContent` were replicated against real playground positions/DOM rects across a 2-cell block — padding/gutter/gap → **null (sticky)**, cell content → inner paragraph, top-level unchanged; the **grip widget DOM was live-verified** in the playground (renders in both default and `data-grid="true"` modes, `position:absolute` so it doesn't disturb the equal-width cells, `draggable`/`contenteditable=false`/SVG present, no console errors). ⚠ Synthetic `mousemove`/`dragstart` do NOT drive the plugin's hover-tracking or a real DnD in the headless preview, so the live hover→drag and grip→drag gestures still need a human pass. Editor-only, no serialize/renderer/bundle change, no redeploy.
   - **Remaining:** the broader variable-block-sizing vision.
   - **Variable block sizing** (the strategic goal this container was the foundation for — differing-width/height blocks, reflow-safe only, no free canvas).
   - **Foldable verification** with a real columns block: `measure.ts` walks `.activity-section > *`, so a top-level container is measured/packed whole by `paginate` — confirm it doesn't split mid-container and its inner width resolves against the panel width.
   - **Soft-deprecate the per-mode `Columns` print controls — DONE (UI-only removal; committed `4a2cb76`).** Now that structural authored columns render in every output, the legacy worksheet `column-count` print setting is redundant. Removed both author-facing controls — the "Columns" dropdown in `ActivityEditor.tsx`'s print disclosure and the session-override "Columns" select in `ActivityPrint.tsx` (which also retires the `layout !== 'foldable'` hide-in-foldable guard, since there's no longer a control to hide). **Deliberately UI-only:** the underlying plumbing is kept dormant — `PrintConfig.columns` (schema), the `--print-columns` container var (`renderer/src/document.ts`), and its `column-count: var(--print-columns,1)` `@media print` rule (`renderer/src/runtime/styles.ts`) all remain, each annotated with a DORMANT note explaining why. Rationale: (a) activities already saved with `columns:2/3` keep printing as authored (hiding the control doesn't neutralize stored values — the renderer CSS is untouched on purpose), and (b) the feature can be re-exposed later with just the dropdown, no schema/renderer/redeploy churn. New activities default to `columns:1` (a no-op). **No renderer behavior change → no bundle regen / redeploy needed.** Schema 45 / renderer 235 / app 106 tests + app build all green.

   - Summary (default) / All-attempts toggle; summary table (student · latest% · best% · attempt count · last submitted) with expandable per-student drill-down; all-attempts view groups every row by student then attempt #.
   - Drill-down maps each `BlankResponse` to its problem prompt + answer key, shows answer/correct✓✗/confidence per blank and per-section `checkpointResults`; blanks removed by a later edit are labelled "No longer in this activity"; unreadable `responses` jsonb is caught per-row (migrateSubmissionResponses).
   - Entry points: "Submissions" link in the editor header ([ActivityEditor.tsx](packages/app/src/routes/ActivityEditor.tsx)) and per-row on `/activities` ([Activities.tsx](packages/app/src/routes/Activities.tsx) — row refactored from a single wrapping `<Link>` to a flex container so there's no nested anchor).

   **Settled design:**
   - **Per-activity dashboard** at route `/activity/:id/submissions`, reached from a "Submissions" button in the editor header and a per-row link on `/activities`. Submissions are activity-scoped; a global cross-activity view is deferred.
   - **All-attempts toggle, two modes:** *Summary* (default — one row per student: headline score, attempt count, last-submitted) and *All attempts* (every submission row, grouped by student then `attempt_number`).
   - **Summary headline score = latest attempt (primary), best shown secondarily.** ⚠ **CHANGED from original plan** (was "best-score-plus-count"). Rationale: for formative work the latest revision reflects current understanding better than the historical best; best is still displayed, just not the headline.
   - **Student grouping key = exact `display_name` string** (Phase 1 link-share only; `opaque_token` null until Phase 3). Accepted limitation: "Bob S" vs "Bobby Smith" become two students. Roster-canonical identity dedup is Phase 3, not now.
   - **Drill-down:** expand a submission to show per-blank `answer` / correct✓✗ / `confidence`, plus per-section `checkpointResults`. Blank IDs are **mapped to their prompt/answer-key by indexing the exact activity version the student answered** (each submission pins `activity_version_id`; the post-Stage-16 hardening pass added this — see Current focus) so the teacher sees legible problems, and the right answer key even after a republish; legacy/null rows fall back to the current version. No cross-student aggregation ("78% got #3") — that stays Phase 2.
   - **`activityType` filter DROPPED from Stage 16.** ⚠ **CHANGED from original plan** (STATE #6 previously said "Filter by `activityType`"). A per-activity dashboard has one activityType, so the filter is redundant; it only makes sense on a global cross-activity view, which is deferred. Revisit if/when that global view lands.
   - **CSV export deferred** (not in the first cut) to keep scope tight; easy additive follow-on.

7. **Housekeeping (parallel):**
   - `init.test.ts` coverage for `state.blanks` and `state.blocks` initialization (Session 1 leftover)
   - Stage 13.5 popover state machine tests
   - UX validation with 2–3 other teachers on Stage 13 + Stage 13.5 patterns
   - CI workflow: GitHub Actions running `pnpm test` + `pnpm --filter @activity/renderer typecheck` + `pnpm --filter @activity/app build` + `pnpm run bundle:renderer` on push. (Higher priority now: Stage 14 found the renderer runtime test suite had silently never run because `vitest.config.ts` `include` excluded `src/**`, and a committed `tsc -b` break in the app went unnoticed because nothing ran `build`. CI would have caught both.)

8. **Phase 1 polish (after Stage 16 closes the MVP loop):**
   - Markdown paste import
   - Print feature (teacher-configurable printables — see `docs/design/print-and-printables.md`)
   - Custom domain for R2 (when you own a domain)

## Rolling "Last updated" log

---

**Last updated (2026-06-17):** **Delete activities — RLS soft-delete bug fixed (migration 0008; deployed + verified).** The per-row Delete shipped 2026-06-16 with a client soft-delete (`update activities set deleted_at = now()`) that never actually worked under RLS: Postgres enforces the SELECT policy against the *post-update* row, and `activities_select_own` gates on `deleted_at is null`, so writing the timestamp made the new row invisible to SELECT and the UPDATE was rejected with *"new row violates row-level security policy for table activities"* (no `WITH CHECK` change fixes it; `publish_activity` was spared only because it never touches `deleted_at`). Root cause isolated with synthetic RLS probes (temp tables, rolled back) against the live Postgres engine — the error persisted even at `WITH CHECK (true)` and vanished only when the SELECT policy's `deleted_at` clause was removed. Fixed with a `SECURITY DEFINER` RPC `soft_delete_activity(uuid)` (migration 0008): owner-checked via `can_edit_activity`, audit-logged, mirroring `publish_activity`; runs as `postgres` (BYPASSRLS overrides FORCE RLS), bypassing the SELECT-on-new-row check while keeping the strict policy intact so deleted rows stay hidden from clients. `Activities.tsx` now calls `supabase.rpc('soft_delete_activity', …)`. Migration deployed by the author and delete confirmed working end to end. Durable reasoning in DECISIONS → "Activity deletion — soft-delete via SECURITY DEFINER RPC". Commit `5f4d8f8`.

**Last updated (2026-06-16/17):** **Markdown paste import — COMPLETE + hardened (app-only; no deploy).** Paste-markdown → editor blocks via a standalone converter `lib/markdownToTiptap.ts` (markdown-it, lazy-loaded + vanilla) behind a header **Import dialog** (live block/problem summary, warnings, **Copy AI prompt**). Covers headings, paragraphs, bold/italic/code, nested lists, `{{blanks}}`→`fillInBlank`, `{checkpoint}`→checkpoint section break, `$…$`/`$$…$$` math, `![alt](url)` images; a blank-bearing list flattens to one problem per item; unsupported input degrades to text + warning. Blanks/`{checkpoint}` resolved in the mapper; **math lifted from raw source before markdown-it** so LaTeX backslashes survive CommonMark escaping; `![](url)` lifted into block-level images. App-only/additive — no schema/renderer/runtime change, no bundle, no deploy. Also shipped: the agent-facing [format spec](markdown-import-format.md) + canonical prompt (the model wraps its reply in a fence so chat shows a Copy button → raw markdown; the importer unwraps an outer fence), a **drift guard** binding prompt↔doc↔converter, and a StrictMode RTL regression test for the dialog (the repo's first component-test infra). **Two bug fixes from real-use reports:** (1) the dialog vanished on open — FocusTrap `onDeactivate: onClose` fires on StrictMode's dev double-unmount; close is now driven only by Escape + backdrop handlers (regression-tested); (2) a reported *disabled* Import button traced to a stale dev server — the editor-instance lift (`onEditorReady`) was hardened to report only a non-null editor and never null on cleanup. 69 markdown-import tests. Verified against the live ProseMirror schema on `/playground` and end-to-end by the author in the real `ActivityEditor` route (import persists across reload, appends to existing content, published pages render imported math/blanks). Durable reasoning in DECISIONS → "Markdown import".

**Last updated (2026-06-16):** **End-to-end submit path verified + payload bug fixed.** The first real live-network submit found the runtime POSTing camelCase (`activityId`/`displayName`) while `ingest-submission` requires snake_case (`activity_id`/`display_name`) → `400 activity_id is required`. Fixed: the runtime now sends snake_case (canonical — matches DB columns + RPC), with payload assembly extracted to a pure, unit-tested `buildSubmissionPayload`; the renderer bundle was regenerated and `publish-activity` redeployed + the `[TEST] E2E Coverage` activity re-published. Verified the submission row in the DB: `display_name`, `score 0.75`, `attempt_number 1`, `schemaVersion 2`, 4 blanks, 2 checkpoint sections, per-blank confidence all landed. Seed bed: `scripts/seed-e2e-activity.sql`. (A suspected popover flush-leak on the immediate-close path was also investigated this session → confirmed no data loss.) Remaining e2e bits not yet exercised: the **locked-mode** pass (inputs freeze after check) and the in-app **Submissions dashboard** view.

**Last updated (2026-06-13):** **Variable block sizing — COMPLETE + deployed (publish-activity v34); plus two editor UX fixes.** The strategic goal the structural-columns container was built toward: reflow-safe block sizing (no free canvas). Design pass with the author captured in `docs/design/variable-block-sizing.md`; durable decisions in DECISIONS.md → "Structural columns + variable block sizing". Shipped in four drops (one cancelled). **Drop 1 — foundation (`061d696`).** Shared `sizing.ts` fragment: optional `width` (fraction `(0,1]`) + `align` (`left|center|right`, default center, omit-when-default) on image + math_block; `Column.minHeight` (rem). Renderer emits `block-sized` class + `--block-width`/`--cell-min-height` custom properties + `data-block-align` (narrow screens relax widths to 100%, print keeps them); Tiptap attrs + `serialize.ts` round-trip both directions with bounds guards. Additive ⇒ no `schemaVersion`/`STORAGE_SCHEMA_VERSION` bump, no migration; runtime never reads it (presentational, additive RUNTIME.md contract entries). **Drop 2 — column divider drag-resize: BUILT THEN CANCELLED by author (`034e708` add, `2f5b180` remove).** A widget-decoration divider (pair-preserving reweighting, ratio snapping, single-transaction commit) competed for the same gutter strip as the inner-block drag handle and didn't feel reliable under a real mouse — removed; **width presets remain the column-width system** (schema/serialize untouched, `columnResize.ts` recoverable from git). The lesson — *lead with reliable controls, drag only where it proves out* — shaped the rest of the arc (memory: `prefer-reliable-controls-over-drag-gestures`). **Drop 3 — image resize (`2f5b180`, `254f38c`, `0aa0f7e`).** Popover width chips + alignment buttons as the reliable baseline + side drag-handles on the live preview (snap 25/33/50/66/75/100%, Alt = fine-grained, Escape cancels, one commit/drag); the NodeView previews width/align exactly as published. Extended same-day with **free height**: optional `ImageBlock.height` (rem); when width × height disagree with the natural ratio the image **center-crops (`object-fit: cover`), never stretches** (author chose crop); Auto/value height field + bottom-edge drag-handle. Then split **Auto** (`null` = natural size, never upscaled) from **100%** (`width:1` = explicit fill) after the author flagged the original "Full"-means-Auto labeling as confusing. **Drop 4 — cell reserved work space (`f3f98f1`).** `Column.minHeight` exposed via a **"Cell height" toolbar dropdown** (CellHeightControl.tsx: Auto / 4-8-12rem presets / numeric rem input) driving a new `setColumnMinHeight` command — control-first, no drag; editor-only (renderer already consumed `--cell-min-height` from Drop 1). **Two editor UX fixes alongside (`034e708`):** sticky toolbar on long documents (`overflow-hidden` removed from the editor card since it silently disables `position:sticky`); and no more scroll-to-top when selecting an image low in the page (Image + Blank popovers now mount only after their anchor resolves, stay invisible until floating-ui positions them, and focus with `preventScroll`). Final tests: schema 54 / renderer 248 / app 128; `tsc -b` + app build green. ⚠ Standing human-GUI-pass item: the image resize drag-handles (side = width, bottom = height) under a real mouse — toolbar controls/chips fully verified. **Prior:** **Columns polish — drag-between-cells + legacy print-columns cleanup — COMPLETE (code + tests; DOM-verified in playground; two commits).** Two finishing touches to the structural-columns arc. **(1) Drag-between-cells (`6c946de`).** Authors can drag a block from one column cell into another (and reorder within a cell) via the inner-block hover handle, and move the whole columns block via a dedicated grip. The hover handle runs `@tiptap/extension-drag-handle-react` in nested mode scoped by one custom rule (`dragHandleNested.ts`, `defaultRules:false` + `columnsOnlyDrill` + `edgeDetection:'none'`); the hard part was reaching the handle without the target flipping to the container as the cursor crosses the cell padding/gap — solved by yielding no candidate in those bands so the plugin keeps its locked inner node. The columns container is never a hover target (excluded everywhere) — its move is owned by an always-present grip widget decoration (`columnsGrip` plugin in `Columns.ts`) that seeds a native PM node drag and works in both default and grid-lines modes, so the earlier geometric "rail" was removed entirely. Pure rule core unit-tested (7 tests); grip DOM live-verified both modes. ⚠ The live hover→drag / grip→drag *gestures* still need a human pass (synthetic events don't drive the plugin in headless preview). **(2) Legacy print-columns control retired (`4a2cb76`).** The per-mode worksheet `column-count` print setting is redundant now that structural columns render everywhere, so both author-facing controls were removed (the `ActivityEditor.tsx` dropdown + the `ActivityPrint.tsx` session-override, with its hide-in-foldable guard). **UI-only on purpose:** `PrintConfig.columns`, the `--print-columns` var, and the `column-count` `@media print` rule stay dormant (each annotated) so already-saved `columns:2/3` values keep printing and the control can be re-exposed with no schema/renderer change. New activities default to 1 (no-op); **no renderer behavior change → no bundle regen / redeploy.** Schema 45 / renderer 235 / app 106 tests + app build all green. ROADMAP/RUNTIME unchanged (no phase decision moved; both features fall outside RUNTIME's published-runtime scope). Prior: **Structural authored columns + grid lines — COMPLETE (code + tests; browser-verified in playground).** A side-by-side container block (`ColumnsBlock` 2–6 `Column`s, optional per-column `width` weight, columns-in-columns forbidden) that renders identically on screen, in worksheet print, and inside a foldable panel — replacing the per-mode CSS `Columns` print setting with a *content* primitive. Renderer emits a CSS grid driven by a `--columns-template` var (media-query-overridable; collapses to one column under 640px screen only); nested problems number column-major off the shared `nextProblemNumber` closure. Editor uses native Tiptap `Columns`/`Column` nodes (renderHTML/parseHTML + CSS, **not** a React NodeView — avoids the Stage-13.5 reconciliation hazard), inserted via slash menu + toolbar, with `serialize.ts` round-trip. **Grid-lines option** (author asked for "cells + dividers" + "both"): a per-block tri-state (`gridLines: inherit|on|off`) plus an activity-wide print default (`meta.print.gridLines`); renderer emits `data-grid-lines` and boxes the block + rules between cells and between stacked blocks, with ruled cells `align-items:stretch` so the divider spans the full box (fixed a truncated-divider bug on empty cells). Editor adds a contextual "Grid: auto/on/off" toolbar toggle + a print-settings checkbox; `Columns.configure({ gridLinesDefault })` threads the default into the preview. Two commits — schema+renderer `ee3c544`, editor+grid-lines `e16d3b3`. Additive schema (no `schemaVersion` bump, no migration). Tests schema 45 / renderer 235 / app 83, app build clean. ⚠ **`publish-activity` redeploy required** — the regenerated bundle is committed but the function imports it, so the author runs `supabase functions deploy publish-activity` for the grid CSS to reach published pages. ## Retention-arc verification narratives (2026-08-04, moved from STATE)

*0024 verification, run 2026-08-04 after the author applied it — all green.* FK is SET NULL, `actor_id` still nullable, `audit_log` has left the eligibility guard, the `actor_purged` stamp is present, DEFINER + `search_path=public` intact, grants still `service_role` + `postgres` only. **C1 returned `user_rows_left=0 | stamped=1 | null_actors_now=8` — an account genuinely deleted for the first time on this project**, its audit row surviving and marked; C2 confirms clean rollback (3 users, 165 audit rows, 7 null actors, 0 stamped). D2 confirms the three teacher-side NO ACTION FKs are preserved. D1 restates the one remaining gap: 0 soft-deleted accounts, because nothing starts the clock.

*0023 verification, run 2026-08-04 after the author applied it — all green.* Section 0 confirms it live; A1–A3 pass (deletes the `auth.users` row, the old `delete from users where deleted_at` line is GONE, blocked accounts skipped and counted, DEFINER + `search_path=public` intact); B1 grants still `service_role` + `postgres` only; B2 the FK layout the ruling rests on is unchanged (`submissions.student_id` RESTRICT, `section_checks.student_id` CASCADE, `users.id→auth.users` CASCADE, `audit_log.actor_id` NO ACTION). **C1 — the worst case, every account marked past its window — returned `job SURVIVED (no abort) | users_remaining=3`**, where the pre-0023 function aborted with `23503`; C2 confirms it rolled back clean. D1 restates the live blocker: 3 accounts, 3 blocked by their own audit trail. D2: nothing sets `users.deleted_at`, so the clock never starts. Two round-trips were lost to verify scripts reporting a PASS (and then a not-applied migration) as scary errors; both scripts now open with a section 0 precondition gate.

*0022 verification, run 2026-08-04 after the author applied it — all green.* `verify-0022.sql` A1–A3 / B1–B2 / C1–C2 / D1 all pass: checks are deleted before `activity_versions`, both explicit deletes present with row counting, DEFINER + `search_path=public` intact, grants still `service_role` + `postgres` only, and the `activity_version_id` RESTRICT still in place (it is load-bearing, not collateral). **C1 — the reproduction — returned `purge SUCCEEDED | checks_remaining=0 | activity_rows_left=0`, and rolled back clean** (44 checks intact, activity present, not soft-deleted). Note for next time: C1 reports a PASS as a Postgres `P0001` **error**, because the `raise exception` is what forces the rollback. That looked like a failure on first read, so the script now says so in a banner and the message itself begins "EXPECTED ROLLBACK — this 'error' IS the pass".

## S7 analytics-census verification narratives (2026-08-04/05, moved from STATE 2026-08-05)

All of S7's apply/deploy steps closed; kept here because the *findings* are the lesson, not the ticks.

**Migration 0026 applied + verified live (2026-08-04, immediately after `supabase db push`).** Migrations run through 0026; `get-activity` at v8 with `verify_jwt:false` (flag survived the deploy). The function-ahead-of-migration window closed — while open, the failure behaved exactly as designed (census error → cache row withheld → student still served a correct 200), and edge logs show no content read even reached it.

**verify-0026 green, structural and behavioural.** A1–A3 (2 CASCADE FKs, 0 ledger FKs, 3 DEFINER functions with pinned `search_path`), B1–B4 (RLS forced + zero policies on all three tables, no anon/PUBLIC grant, write paths unreachable by `authenticated`, no student-identifier column), and all of C on its own rolled-back fixture: census 2 counts / 3 items, re-run idempotent, **`fill_in_blank` 4/6 across attempts vs 3/4 on latest** (the re-check inflation that got rollups deferred, demonstrated on real infrastructure), `multiple_choice` 2/3 vs 2/2, one `_unattributed` verdict with a null `block_count`, totals 3 checks / 2 students / censused, stranger AND student both refused with `Not available`, one ledger row written. Rollback confirmed clean. One real defect fixed in the committed script: `activities.slug` is NOT NULL and the fixture omitted it.

**Census backfill (2026-08-05): 24 versions censused** (259 census rows, 225 item rows), 0 write failures, re-run idempotent. All 3 live published activities censused (13 / 17 / 3 keys). **110 versions SKIPPED as unservable** — every one `schemaVersion 1` with no upgrade path (superseded history plus the 13 soft-deleted activities' versions). Not new breakage: the read API cannot serve those either (500 `upgrade_failed`), and no live published activity is among them. It is the concrete form of R6(d). Two real script defects found by running it: it imported `@supabase/supabase-js`, which pnpm cannot resolve from the workspace root (now plain `fetch`, zero deps), and it counted unservable skips as FAILURES, so a healthy run exited 1 and read as an outage.

**Live e2e probe (2026-08-05): `verify-analytics-e2e.js` 8 PASS / 0 FAIL**, signed in. Covers the layer nothing else reaches: resolve + content read still 200 with sections, no census/analytics field in the served document (8728 bytes scanned), owner reads `get_activity_analytics` over real HTTP with the documented shape, non-owned activity refused (`P0001 Not available`), anon refused by the grant (401), current version reports censused=true / 13 keys / 0 checks. Session-free legs verified by curl (META 200 anonymous with `teacher_name:null`; CONTENT 401 without auth); the deployed Pages bundle carries the panel route.

**Two copy defects the probe surfaced, both fixed** — and the reason a learning was logged: the probe said "maintenance job has NEVER run — schedule the cron" and the panel said "the nightly analytics job is not scheduled yet". Both went false the moment the cron was registered, and both would have sent a reader to re-schedule an already-scheduled job. The ledger knows whether the job RAN; it knows nothing about whether it is SCHEDULED. Both now say only that, and the panel gained a real staleness signal instead (a run older than 36h reads "may have stopped").

**E2E residue cleared 2026-08-04.** The 44 `section_checks` rows on activity `6a84c8cb` are gone; the activity, its 4 versions, users and audit_log verified untouched. That deletion would have quietly broken `verify-0022.sql` C1, which pointed at those ambient rows — with no check to block, its "purge SUCCEEDED, checks_remaining=0" would have passed while proving nothing about the RESTRICT it exists to test. C1 now builds its own fixture and asserts `checks_before=1`, proven both ways against the live DB (passes on the current function, still reports `23503` when the pre-0022 ordering is reinstalled).

**0021 verification (2026-08-04) — all green.** `verify-0021.sql` A1–A3 / B1 / C1–C2 pass (trigger carries no email fallback, RPC guard present, grants survived REPLACE, 0 fallback artifacts, 0 leaking activities). Regression re-runs clean: `verify-0017.sql` A1–A3 / B1–B2 / D1, `verify-0013-0014.sql` A1–A7 / B1–B6. Live over HTTP with no `Authorization` header, the two email-backed activities return `teacher_name:null` with titles intact. Security advisor shows no NEW findings. One script fix fell out: `verify-0013-0014.sql` B5 said "EXPECT 5" while querying both migrations' policies — 0014's own `classes_insert_teacher` makes it 6.

**0025 verification (2026-08-04) — closes the retention arc.** A1 dormancy present and students-only; **A2 the job does NOT write `users.deleted_at`** and A3 `join_class` still gates on it — together the pair that keeps a between-terms student out of a lockout; A4/B1 posture and grants unchanged. C1's six-case fixture matrix: `active_member=1 ended_401d=0 ended_100d=1 never_401d=0 never_10d=1 teachers_left=3` — an active membership protects, 401 days dormant purges, 100 days (the summer case) does not, never-joined ages out, a new never-joined account is safe, teachers untouched. C2 confirms every fixture rolled back. D1 returns 0 rows — no student accounts exist yet.

## S8 perf-budget CI (2026-08-05, moved from STATE 2026-08-05)

Eng review ruled 14 decisions (11 + 3 long-term refinements), 0 unresolved; outside voice raised 12 findings, 5 became ruled tensions. Built the same day in two commits.

**The split (D4).** Entry chunk **2.86 MB → 592 KB raw (168.1 KiB gz)**, entry CSS 156 KB → 59 KB raw (11.3 KiB gz). `StudentViewer` + `Home` stay statically imported so the entry chunk IS the student shell — one fetch to first paint, and a cap that measures what a student downloads rather than a router skeleton no journey corresponds to. Before it, a student answering a worksheet downloaded Tiptap, the image cropper, and every authoring surface.

**Calibration honesty.** Ruling P1A sketched ~150 KiB gz; reality is 168.1, and the gap is react-dom + react-router + supabase-js + the viewer's eager tier, not slack. The committed cap is a regression pin at the real number, with the 150 target kept alive as a TODO rather than quietly redefined.

**What the outside voice caught that the review missed.** (1) The route-mount smoke as first ruled was VACUOUS — every teacher route sits behind `RequireAuth`, which redirects an unauthenticated visit, so the smoke would have passed on six broken chunks; it now signs in and asserts each route's lazy chunk was actually fetched. (2) CDP network throttling is not reliably applied to service-worker-mediated fetches, and the prod build registers a worker immediately — so the lane asserts a fresh context and `workerStart === 0` on every measured resource, or the numbers are fiction. (3) The KaTeX question was a false binary (see below).

**Four defects found by running things, all in the test code.** An ambiguous `Loading…` selector that could not tell a route's own loading state from the Suspense fallback (ActivityPrint failed on a working chunk → added `data-route-fallback`); a periodic fixture filler that gzip crushed, so three over-cap cases silently landed under cap (→ `crypto.randomBytes`); the student path fetching pwa-register/workbox chunks off the render path, breaking a naive "exactly one chunk" assertion; and `.viewer-section` being rendered by the loading Skeleton too, so the timing spec could read a mark before the worksheet existed and report it as never stamped (→ wait on `.viewer-topbar`).

**T7 resolved by measurement, not argument.** Preload-on-math-detect halved the LaTeX-fallback window **~737 ms → ~382 ms** (runs: 384/384/379/383), math typeset ~320 ms sooner, for a ~40 ms cost to worksheet-interactive. Eager loading rejected: +45% first load on every math page to close a sub-half-second window. Full reasoning in DECISIONS → "Preload math on detect".

## S9 cutover arc — closing narratives (archived from STATE 2026-08-15 late)

**🏁 THE S9 CUTOVER ARC IS COMPLETE.** All five drops shipped, every author station ran (0027→0032 applied, three Edge Functions deleted, the integration lane's first green run, the R2 bucket gone), and the **final C1–C15 gate sweep was run against shipped reality** — 14 closed, 1 standing, gate 4 deliberately deferred to the first classroom because it needs a real district domain and nothing else waits on it. **R2 is absent end to end: no code path, no script, no secret, no bucket.** The student path is now: viewer at `/a/:id`, server-authoritative grading, live-API reads, offline-capable — with no published-HTML world behind it. **The sweep earned its keep on its last pass**, catching STATE's Edge Function versions as the `entrypoint_path` misread for the second time in the arc (corrected under Baseline facts, with the procedural fix). What remains is not cutover work: gate 4 when a classroom approaches, the D24 counsel read before real students, and the admission-model re-ruling ([admission-model.md](docs/design/admission-model.md)) at the catalog kickoff.

**2026-08-14 late — **The S9 arc is down to two author decisions and the teardown steps.** Same-day close-outs: the integration lane's first green run (3/3 — after finding the chain unreplayable, the platform-default grant gap, and its own three stub assertions; 0031+0032 applied live as verified no-ops, 0031 now carrying live's `rls_auto_enable` byte-for-byte, md5-verified); gate 9's recalibration + s8-retro re-run (both by their pre-ruled procedures); the R2 station's two pre-checks — which found and fixed a real production defect: **math fields on the deployed app were 404ing their MathLive glyph fonts** (the app-bundled kit derives a chunk-sibling fonts URL that didn't exist in dist; the build now self-hosts the 20 woff2 files at that exact path, version-guarded — `d9a93f6`, must deploy before the bucket dies). Live: migrations through **0032**, two Edge Functions, `verify:auth --target live` 85/0. What remains: the 150 KiB ruling, gate 4's seeding, the D-13b disposability ruling, and the R2 dashboard/bucket steps — every one needs the author. Full narratives in [HISTORY.md](docs/HISTORY.md).

**The session's own finding, and why it matters more than its size:** the a11y lane went red on its first real CI run, and **the fix for it went red again for the same reason** — a "wait for the lazy blocks to mount" guard that counted markers which cannot exist before the chunk resolves, so it skipped the wait entirely and scanned the pre-mount DOM. Local passed; CI didn't. Underneath that vacuity sat a real WCAG A defect: **MathLive gives its keyboard sink `role="textbox"` with no accessible name, so a student's math answer box announced as an unlabeled textbox** — fixed by naming the shadow node, since 0.109.2 exposes no API for it. Its sibling `nested-interactive` is MathLive's own structure and is carved out by rule AND element, with a row that asserts the carve-out is still load-bearing so it self-retires. The failure-matrix tab-lock row failed a **second** time and is no longer a flake: the winner was snapshotted mid-settle and could move before the fill; it now requires two agreeing samples.

**Then the same lesson landed a third time, which is the part worth keeping.** The push went GREEN (run 31791526509 — OV-DX-8 closed), but with 2 flaky, and they were *not* the fixed row: they were its two `two tabs` siblings, holding byte-identical copies of the same idiom. Fixing the row CI happened to fail on had left the class intact, and it simply surfaced one row over. All four rows now share one `settledLockHolders()` helper, including the fourth that had not flaked yet — leaving one copy behind is how this returns a fourth time. **None of the three sightings reproduce locally, so the flaky count is the only real evidence** — and the next run came back **71 passed / 0 flaky** (31795715961). One clean run is corroboration rather than proof; the strong half of the argument is structural, that the idiom no longer exists in any of the four rows.

## The teacher-grading slice: designed, reviewed, built, applied (2026-08-15 → 16)

Archived from STATE at session end. The slice went from an unparked TODOS entry
to live-and-CI-green in one arc: a P10 re-derivation, an eng review (G1–G14) and
a design review (§2b/G8-DR, 4/10 → 9/10) each with an independent outside voice,
then T1–T6 built, migration 0034 applied, pushed, and green on its first CI run.

**Author station, 2026-08-16.** 0034 applied; `pnpm verify:auth --target live` =
**116 passed / 0 failed across 10 scripts**. Live scores 116 where a fresh local
DB scores 114/2 — the two extra passes are verify-image-storage and verify-0020,
whose preconditions need a seeded published activity that live has and a reset
local DB does not. The local "failures" were never defects, which is worth
remembering the next time a fresh-DB run looks red.

**The posture was re-verified against live rather than against that number:**
migrations through 0034 · `check_grades` present with RLS enabled AND forced,
**zero policies**, no client table grants · `grades` + `can_grade_submission`
gone · all four RPCs present and anon-denied · `check_id` CASCADE +
`graded_by` SET NULL · `grade.release` in the enum · the readback's body carries
no `activity_versions` reference (the leak guard) · the upsert gates on
`can_edit_activity` and never `can_read_activity`.

**The purge rewrite got a LIVENESS proof, not a source check (P3).** §G exists
because a purge job citing a dropped relation fails at its NEXT NIGHTLY FIRE,
not at migration time, so waiting for 03:00 UTC would have been the wrong kind
of confidence. `purge_soft_deleted()` was executed end to end against live data
inside an aborted transaction (the verify-0025/0033 EXPECTED-ROLLBACK idiom): it
ran clean, and row counts after (8 activities / 24 versions / 3 users / 1 class)
confirmed nothing was durably deleted. ⚠ Historical note for that table, so a
future reader does not misread it: jobid 1 FAILED on 2026-08-14 03:00 with a
`submissions_activity_id_fkey` violation — that predates 0029's wipe the same
day, and the 08-15 run succeeded. Resolved, not lurking.

**The push silently did not land the first time.** `origin/main` stayed at
`68e4d1e` while local was 7 ahead, and nothing else looked wrong: clean tree,
green tests, migration live. The only stale thing was the deployed app being
older than everything else. `git ls-remote origin refs/heads/main` is the check
that catches it; `git status` reports "ahead N" whether or not a push was
attempted. Second attempt landed at `27986f8`.

**CI green on the slice's first run** ([31893704928](https://github.com/ZanReed/activity-platform/actions/runs/31893704928)):
unit suites identical to local (schema 340 / graph-kit 384 / viewer 1139 / app
1077), 12/12 perf budgets with both bundle-drift guards passing — `wire.ts`
feeds the grading-server bundle, so the regeneration committed with T3 is what
kept this from failing CI — print gates 83, and the four e2e lanes at **73
passed, 0 flaky**, up from 72: exactly the one a11y row the slice added. A count
that did not reconcile would have meant a phantom or a lost row; green alone
would not have said so.
